*查證備註：* The overall approach is confirmed. server_relay on the peer is unverified on the class page. If it errors, set multiplayer.server_relay (SceneMultiplayer) instead. In a host-authoritative design every message goes through the host anyway, so relay matters little.

來源：<https://godotsteam.com/classes/multiplayer_peer/>、<https://godotsteam.com/tutorials/multiplayer_peer/>、<https://godotsteam.com/changelog/multiplayer_peer/>、<https://godotsteam.com/blog/category/multiplayerpeer/>

### [confirmed] The lobby, invite and join API names and signal signatures given are current as of 4.23.

Confirmed against the Matchmaking class reference. createLobby(LobbyType, max_members): the maximum is 250. LobbyType enum: PRIVATE=0, FRIENDS_ONLY=1, PUBLIC=2, INVISIBLE=3, PRIVATE_UNIQUE=4. Signals: lobby_created(connect: Result, lobby); lobby_joined(lobby_id, permissions (unused, always 0), locked, response); lobby_chat_update(lobby_id, changed_id, making_change_id, chat_state); lobby_match_list(Array); lobby_data_update(success: bool, lobby_id, member_id). The lobbies tutorial confirms that join_requested fires for in-game accepts, that a cold start passes '+connect_lobby <id>', that activateGameOverlayInviteDialog(lobby_id) opens the invite dialog, and that the overlay has been 'wonky since Vulkan', so test launched from the Steam client. The tutorial cites Valve's Spacewar for its kick approach, which uses a host message rather than a native kick, matching the 'no built-in kick' claim.

*查證備註：* The tutorial's hosting check is 'if connect == 1' (Result OK = 1), which is the same as Steam.RESULT_OK.

來源：<https://godotsteam.com/classes/matchmaking/>、<https://godotsteam.com/tutorials/lobbies/>

### [unverifiable] Spacewar (480) limitations: lobby searches are polluted, achievements and stats are shared, and cold-start invites likely launch Spacewar.

Partly confirmed. The lobbies tutorial says AppID 480 has 'tons of other test and junk lobbies' and recommends a search tag. The filter API names are confirmed: addRequestLobbyListStringFilter, addRequestLobbyListDistanceFilter (CLOSE/DEFAULT/FAR/WORLDWIDE), addRequestLobbyListResultCountFilter and LOBBY_COMPARISON_EQUAL. The default result cap of about 50 and the claim that filters reset after each request match Valve's Steamworks behaviour but were not re-verified on GodotSteam pages. The claim that a cold-start 'Join Game' launches Spacewar has no source and remains an inference. The $100 Steam Direct fee is well established.

*查證備註：* Points 1, 2, 4 and 5 are sound. Point 3 (cold-start invites launching Spacewar) needs a test with two accounts. Until then, tell friends to start the game first and then accept the invite.

來源：<https://godotsteam.com/tutorials/lobbies/>、<https://godotsteam.com/classes/matchmaking/>

### [confirmed] Architecture: run a host-authoritative listen server behind a swappable transport layer (Steam / ENet / Offline), and keep saves on the host.

This is a design recommendation, not a factual claim. The facts it relies on are correct. OfflineMultiplayerPeer is Godot 4's default peer, with unique ID 1. ENetMultiplayerPeer has create_server and create_client. Swapping multiplayer.multiplayer_peer changes the transport while the RPC code stays the same.

*查證備註：* The design is sound. Combat at 1–2 rounds per second is a choice, not a documented feature of the original game.

來源：<https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/networking/high_level_multiplayer.html>

### [corrected] RPG Maker MZ has no maintained Steam P2P multiplayer path, and the NekoGakuen author recommends nw.js 0.49.2 for Greenworks.

The main conclusion holds: no maintained plugin was found that combines Steam lobbies with P2P networking for MZ. The existing options are SteamLink (paid, Steam integration only), the NekoGakuen SteamworksPlus plugin, a LAN multiplayer plugin, MMORPG Maker (server-based, $40+, early access), QKing's RPG Maker MZ Online (currently unavailable) and Alpha NET Z (status unclear). Correction: the 'nw.js v0.49.2' recommendation comes from a commenter on the SteamLink itch page, not the NekoGakuen author. The NekoGakuen guide (hackmd) only recommends RPG Maker MV core 1.6.2+ and says a Steamworks developer account and a registered app are required.

*查證備註：* Godot remains the right choice. The 0.49.2 attribution was wrong.

來源：<https://undermax.itch.io/steamlink>、<https://hackmd.io/@Mirai/Steamworks_NWjs_eng.md>、<https://rpg-maker-plugins.itch.io/multiplayer>、<https://rmalizia44.itch.io/mmorpg-maker>、<https://qkingsoftware.itch.io/rpgmakermp>

### [confirmed] Data-driven design: keep content in CSV or JSON tables, load them into registries at startup, and use .tres Resources only for small hand-tuned items.

This is a design recommendation. FileAccess.get_csv_line() and JSON.parse_string() exist in Godot 4. Godot's CSV translation import exists. One caveat: by default Godot imports .csv files under res:// as translations. For data CSVs, set the import mode to 'Keep File' (or use another extension) so they ship in the export and get_csv_line() can read them.

*查證備註：* Add this caveat: set data CSV imports to Keep File, or exported builds may be missing them.

來源：<https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/classes/class_fileaccess.html>

### [confirmed] Fonts: LXGW WenKai TC v1.522 (OFL-1.1) plus Noto Serif/Sans TC (OFL-1.1).

v1.522 is confirmed as the latest stable release (2026-03-17). A v1.530-RC pre-release is dated Oct 13. The license file was not shown on the releases page. LXGW fonts are known to use OFL-1.1, and Noto CJK / Source Han are OFL-1.1.

*查證備註：* Check the OFL.txt in the repo before shipping.

來源：<https://github.com/lxgw/LxgwWenkaiTC/releases>

### [confirmed] CC0 asset sources: Kenney, OpenGameArt 'Ancient Chinese Character Pack 1' by nessava, and itch.io's CC0 filter. game-icons.net is CC BY 3.0.

Confirmed. The nessava pack is CC0 with 8 figures (a male and a female each for the Han, Tang, Yuan and Qing dynasties). Kenney is CC0, and game-icons.net is CC BY 3.0 (requires attribution), both well known. The LinXueLian non-commercial status was not re-checked.

*查證備註：* Note that the nessava pack has no Song or Ming figures, the eras closest to the wuxia setting.

來源：<https://opengameart.org/content/ancient-chinese-character-pack-1>

### [confirmed] Installation steps for Godot 4.6.3 plus the GodotSteam GDExtension on Windows, including export.

The steps match the docs: res://addons/godotsteam, Project Settings > Steam > Initialization (4.14+), the Steam client must be running, and the overlay is unreliable under Vulkan. Additions: 4.23.1 adds an in-editor updater (Project > Tools). The store lists Godot 4.4.1 as the minimum. The exact zip filename was not verified.

*查證備註：* The minor details are fine.

來源：<https://godotsteam.com/tutorials/initializing/>、<https://store.godotengine.org/asset/godotsteam/godotsteam-gdextension/>

### 補充

- License risk: in May 2026 the GodotSteam blog announced that 4.20 would drop MIT for a custom GodotSteamKit-style license restricting where it can be shared and how it is used in things that aren't games. The June 24 post said this was 'temporarily scrapped' until closer to Godot 5. The Godot store lists v4.23.1 as MIT. Recheck the license when upgrading GodotSteam.
- GodotSteam v4.23.1 (store changelog dated 2026-10-07) is newer than 4.23. It adds a plugin updater run from Project > Tools. The store marks it unstable and lists Godot 4.4.1 as the minimum.
- Do not install the ExpressoBits steam-multiplayer-peer GDExtension alongside GodotSteam 4.17+. Their class registrations conflict, and ExpressoBits development has paused.
- Godot imports .csv files under res:// as translations by default. Set the import mode of data tables to 'Keep File', or exported builds may be missing them.
- GodotSteamKit 1.1.3 (Oct 5, 2026) is a separate companion plugin that automatically adds a steamworks.gd autoload. It is optional, and its license is more restrictive than GodotSteam's.
- In the GodotSteam tutorial, server_relay is set after create_host() or create_client(). Godot's SceneMultiplayer also exposes multiplayer.server_relay (default true), which may be the property that actually takes effect.

## M3a 門派研究 (8 remaining 1.0 sects + battle-command / 門規 mechanics; researched and adversarially re-checked 2026-10-19)

> How to read: each bullet is `[verdict] claim — era — sources`. Verdicts: **confirmed** (re-fetched and matches), **corrected** (the corrected form is what is written here), **later** (true but from a post-1.0A version: 1.0B 神鵰 2001-09/10, 決戰光明頂 2002-01-25, 喬峰傳奇 2003+, 免費版/懷舊版/至尊版 2006+), **unverifiable** (could not be re-found; plausible), **refuted**. Era caveats that apply everywhere:
> - 17173 2001 player articles come from **mainland** servers (Shanghai gs5/gs8, Guangzhou gs1/gs4, Harbin, Chengdu); official chinesegamer.net pages are **Taiwan** pages captured late (footer mentions 第15片資料片 五嶽劍派), so their content is "official but late-dated".
> - The fan blog jyfreeonline.blogspot.com (2012–2015, 免費版) calls itself a "1.0 資料庫" but includes 2003+ content and private-server (霸主/脫機) scene IDs. jy2001.net/t/30x posts are verbatim copies of it (count once).
> - The 17173 2001-07-05 「選擇門派利弊分析功略」 (also gaoshou/16) uses pre-launch sect-skill names (謙虛值, 陷媚值, 威吓值) and differs from the official proc table for 武當, 血刀, 星宿, 古墓 and 恆山. Treat it as pre-launch design copy, not observed play. **Use the official proc table** (index_jy2-25) and tag conflicts 【待考證】.
> - Sect-skill **combat procs** were switched on with 決戰光明頂 2002-01-25 (news 399 「6. 開放各門派技能功用」). In 2001 sect skills only gated learning.
> - Coordinates: original x/y ÷ 24 = `world_pos` (WORLD_2001 §1.1). 17173 gaoshou/17 gives two readings per gate, e.g. 「終南山 17630,11000(17407,10987)」.
> - Every source below was re-fetched by the checker unless marked unverifiable; raw/decoded copies are in the session scratchpad (`wd/ em/ hs/ qz/ gumu/ lj/ xx/ xd/ fc/`).

### 武當派 (wudang)

- [confirmed] Gate at 20094,9406 → world_pos [837,392], between 華山 (17404,7820) and 少林 (23214,7004), south of the line. The 2001-07-16 guide says the 大理→少林 茶食 trade run 「經過武當山」. 令狐沖 stands at the 華山–武當 pond (17461,8715) only at certain times. — 1.0A — jinyong.17173.com/gaoshou/17.htm, gaoshou/23.htm; 17173 2001-07-16 n317_233956.
- [corrected] Rooms attested: 三清宮 (張三丰's room), 內堂 (kneel to 張三丰 to join), 大殿 with the 踏牆 room behind it (run by 二叔 = 俞蓮舟), 稻田 (農夫), gate with 知客道長; 東超 stands 「進武當後三清宮右邊」; 武當山下 has bandits and weak monsters. A second 稻田 lies outside the gate to the NW (七桃仙 at 19128,8376 「武當外稻田後方山坡上」). Later only: 武當廣場, 劍塚, 舊武當. — 1.0A / later — 17173 mengpai/2, gonglue/other, 2001-07-08 n283_199288, gaoshou/23; jyfreeonline wudangpai.
- [confirmed] Join (2001-07-08 「拜入十大名門派的基本條件」, verbatim): 善惡 ≥ 50, 男性, carrying 引薦書 from the 引薦人 outside the gate, kneel to 張三丰 in 內堂. Refused: 善惡 < 50, female. 17173 sect page: 善良以上, 無門派, 男性, never expelled / no 武當叛徒 mark. GDD's −99 is the 2001-08 betrayal line, not the join line. — 1.0A — 17173 2001-07-08 n32_948265; mengpai/2.
- [later] Female route (公敵 of 星宿/血刀/少林, 歷練 20000, 未婚, 正善) is 免費版 only. Do not implement. — jyfreeonline.
- [confirmed] Gate rules: non-members enter via 知客道長 (「規定時間」, hours unknown); choosing 滅派 there makes battles everywhere in 武當; disciples need 『武當派山令』 to come and go; 善惡為惡 players entering are attacked at once; traitors attacked; 滅派/叛派 gives a hidden 『武當公敵』 mark, never re-enter. The "善惡 ≤ −1000 walk the 大廳 → random 武當弟子 fights" farming note is plausible but its source URL is lost (yinan/28 is the death FAQ). — 1.0A/1.0B — mengpai/2.
- [confirmed] Sect skill 俠義 → 抗技: 「機率抵抗狀態（如毒、麻痺、混亂等…）, 會耗精與內力」. Specific arts need a minimum 俠義. In 2001 quests were the only way to raise 俠義. The fan blog's "random attack up" is 免費版 and conflicts; follow the official text. — official (late page) — jy.chinesegamer.net index_jy2-25; mengpai/2.
- [confirmed] 俠義 quest 剿滅盜匪 / 殲逞令: 玄虛刀法 lv15 → 宋遠橋 gives 殲逞令 → kill bandits (2001-07-07: 「峨嵋山下」; later hint: 過崆峒派右邊的橋往下直走) → bring 首級, talk to 宋遠橋 twice (2nd talk 「教你休息」, meaning unclear 【待考證】). +50 俠義, repeatable, 武當弟子 only. Not yet open on 2001-07-16, open soon after. — 1.0A — 2001-07-07 n47_963631; gonglue/other; 2001-07-16.
- [confirmed] 俠義 quest 好心有好報 (2001-07-18 update): an old man in 大理 (between the vegetable seller and the weapon seller) → deliver 家書 to 東超 → +25 俠義 (blog: 名聲3 歷練4 俠義熟練 20). 武當弟子 only, repeatable. — 1.0A — gonglue/tishi, gonglue/other.
- [confirmed] Six 1.0A quest titles: 剿滅盜匪, 龍門遺案 (→ 真武七截陣), 殷六俠的迷思 (→ 梯雲縱), 真假黑玉斷續膏之謎 (→ 純陽無極功), 倚天屠龍筆法, 太極拳與太極劍. Plus 幫助陳大寶 (2001-07-26; 陳大寶 at 16845,10507 「終南山左上方河邊」). Detailed steps are later-era blog data. — gonglue/1, tishi, gaoshou/23.
- [confirmed] 叛派 triggers 2001-08-16: 善惡 < −99 while dealing with 張三丰/俞岱巖/宋青書/中飛/殷梨亭/俞蓮舟; 善惡 < −199 with 張松溪/莫聲谷/東超/西雲/南羽/北忠/宋遠橋; 同門相殘. 17173 sect page: PKing a fellow disciple or sect NPC = immediate expulsion and loss of 俠義. — 1.0A — 17173 2001-08-16 n315_231423; mengpai/2.
- [confirmed] Official 門規 (from 2001-11-15): 善惡 < −299, 攻擊武當門人, 武當公敵 → 1 day sect jail (online time). Enforcers 張三丰, 宋遠橋, 俞蓮舟, 俞岱巖, 張松溪, 殷梨亭, 莫聲谷…. 叛教 = all skills halved incl. basics, sect skill → 0, 公敵, 3 days jail if caught. — official late — index_jy2-22, 2-23.
- [confirmed] Masters (17173 sect page): 張三丰 純陽無極功, 倚天屠龍筆法; 宋遠橋 真武七截陣 (+閉關 → +10 基本內功 +10 武當心法 per session, no 學點); 俞岱巖 the 8 base arts (武當心法, 武當身法, 武當長拳, 繞指柔劍, 玄虛刀法, 太極神功, 綿掌, 柔雲劍法); 殷梨亭 梯雲縱; 張松溪 太極拳 (+寫書法 → 讀書識字; +1 悟性 at 讀書 11); 俞蓮舟 太極劍法 (+踏牆 輕功). Roster: 張三丰, 宋遠橋, 俞蓮舟, 俞岱巖, 張松溪, 殷梨亭, 莫聲谷, 宋青書, 中飛, 東超, 西雲, 南羽, 北忠, 知客道長, 引薦人, 武當弟子. — 1.0A/1.0B — mengpai/2; 2001-08-16.
- [confirmed] Learning prerequisites (2001-08-07, verbatim): 太極神功 武當心法50; 純陽無極功 悟25 福26 定23 根22; 梯雲縱 定22 根22; 綿掌 武當長拳30; 太極拳 臂26 定22 機22 根22 (+1 unknown); 柔雲劍法 繞指柔劍40; 太極劍法 定26 根28; 倚天屠龍筆法 臂26 悟25; 真武七截陣 悟25 定25 (+1 unknown); 絕戶虎爪手 臂24 定24 根24. No 俠義 numbers. The 1.0 chain is 繞指 → 柔雲 (the 2012 blog reverses it). — 1.0A — 17173 2001-08-07 n928_844200.
- [confirmed] Categories: 內 武當心法/太極神功/純陽無極功; 輕 武當身法/梯雲縱; 拳掌 武當長拳/綿掌/太極拳/絕戶虎爪手; 劍 繞指柔劍/柔雲劍法/太極劍法; 刀 玄虛刀法; 筆 倚天屠龍筆法; 陣 真武七截陣. No 暗器/毒/醫. — 1.0A.
- [confirmed] Attack shapes (17173 gaoshou/32): 繞指柔劍 小十字 (5 cells, any target cell), cast 6–8 s; 武當長拳 小十字; 柔雲劍法 大十字 (「上下左右各三」 but the text says 共10格 → 13 vs 10 conflict 【待考證】), 2–3 s; 玄虛刀法 直線, 3–5 s; 綿掌 直線, ~2–3 s. Level-dependent shapes are later. — 1.0A/1.0B.
- [confirmed] Special arts: quest gives lv1, then self-train ~50–60 h to lv30; coefficients 太極劍 1.43, 太極拳 1.9 (jb71). 消點 for 無極功/梯雲縱/陣法 not yet found in 2001. — gaoshou/jb71, jb46.
- [later] 俠義 thresholds 40–70, level caps 80/100, sect weapons; graduation (實戰100萬, 基內50, 拆招50, 俠義50; 張三豊; rejoin via 知客道長). — jyfreeonline; index_jy2-29-3.
- [confirmed] Sect life: no meal rule; only 農夫 inside (稻田, 精+氣); 踏牆 via 俞蓮舟 (10 氣/attempt; success +20 熟練, fail +10; deliberately failing in ~5 s gives +10 基本輕功 +3 武當身法 vs +15/+5 for a full 30 s success); 寫書法 via 張松溪 (精); 抱太極 sparring with 武當弟子 (精氣神). — 1.0A — mengpai/2; 2001-07-08 (白天天); 2001-07-16.
- [confirmed] Newbie path (2001-07-16): 玄虛刀法 first (unlocks the 俠義 quest at 15), 讀書 to 11 at 張松溪, 武當心法 + 基本內功 ≥ 15, then 繞指柔劍 + 基本劍法; farm 武當山下 bandits (avoid 魔女, 隱面人, 邪派弟子, 不義之士); start in 大理 (the 茶食 run passes 武當). — 1.0A.
- [corrected] Difficulty benchmark (15萬 實戰 for evil farmers at 武當): original URL unknown; medium confidence. — this file, 2002 benchmark list.
- Open: room count/exits (default to the shaolin.json pattern 【待考證】); what 武當派山令 is; 引薦人 conditions; per-art 俠義 thresholds; 抗技 numbers; 1.0 caps; 招式 names (no source names any); 絕戶虎爪手 teacher; minor NPC roles (宋青書, 中飛, 西雲, 南羽, 北忠).

### 峨嵋派 (emei)

- [confirmed] Sect skill 斷惡 → 閃避: 「機率閃避敵人攻擊, 會耗精與神」 (official, late page). 2001-07-05 text: 「拆招卸力發動機率最高…隨機地擋開敵方攻擊」, 斷惡 raised by killing 盜賊 / bandit quests, higher 斷惡 lowers 受傷 chance, drawback: 抗藥性 rises faster. Treat both as one evade-type proc. — official / 1.0A — index_jy2-25, 2-16-3; 17173 2001-07-05 n751_667748.
- [corrected] 斷惡 gain: 「只要每殺一個善惡值為邪惡之人，斷惡值就會有所增長」; training cost 精/神/氣 (17173) vs per-proc cost 精/神 (official) — different things. 斷惡 gates advanced arts. — mengpai/3.
- [confirmed] Gate 13902,13772 → [579,574]. 大理 is the start town (official newbie page grouping, late but consistent with 1.0A). Nearby: 紀曉芙 12879,13843 「峨嵋出口左邊樹林中」; 莹月兒 11696,14786; 平一指 17637,14053 「峨嵋右方，往衡山客棧途中」. — gaoshou/17, 23; index_jy2-6-4.
- [corrected] The 峨嵋迷宮 map is NOT dead: images.17173cdn.com/jinyong/images/ditu/4.jpg — a 10-floor linear maze (entrance floor 1, exit floor 10). 2002 benchmark: ~4萬 實戰 / 特武 lv25. Local copy: scratchpad/em/emei_maze_4.jpg. — 17173 ditu/4.
- [confirmed] Rooms (thin): entrance with 峨嵋弟子 (evil players start fights there), 滅絕's house (the entry point before it is the rejoin point), kitchen (周芷若 cooking, later), 周芷若's room; 萬年寺 and 後山洞 are later. Fan scene IDs 4100–4109 suggest ~7 sect scenes. — index_jy2-29-3; mengpai/3; jyfreeonline ermei.
- [confirmed] Join: 善惡 not 邪惡, 無門派, 女性, not 峨嵋公敵 (no attribute/PK/marriage/test). 專收女性. Male route is 2012-blog only (with a bug note). — 1.0A — mengpai/3; index_jy2-6-4.
- [confirmed] 門規: 2001-08-16 叛派 = 善惡 < −299, 同門相殘 (no PK/殺氣/marriage clause). Below 善良 → masters stop teaching; 邪惡 → expelled; PK of a fellow 峨嵋 player/NPC = expulsion; 公敵 never rejoins and is attacked on entry. Enforcers 滅絕師太, 周芷若, 貝綿儀, 靜玄, 靜迦, 峨嵋弟子. 殺氣 too high blocks learning (禪修 with 蓮香 lowers it). — 1.0A + official — n315_231423; index_jy2-22; mengpai/3.
- [confirmed] Scene access: neutral-or-better non-members enter freely; 邪惡 non-members and 公敵 get battles at any time; leaving is free. — mengpai/3.
- [confirmed] Masters 2001: 滅絕師太 teaches 峨嵋九陽功, 滅劍, 絕劍, 佛光普照掌 (2001-08-13 player post: 滅絕 teaches 九陽/滅劍 **only to lv1**, the rest is self-trained); 靜迦 teaches 峨嵋心法, 峨嵋身法, 飄雪穿雲掌, 金頂綿掌, 迴風拂柳劍. Sparring 對招 with fellow disciples (精/氣/神). — 1.0A — mengpai/3; 2001-08-13 n192_108147.
- [later] Trainers 靜滅 (滅劍 10→90), 靜絕, 周芷若 (九陽 10→80), 靜光 (佛光), 郭襄 (四象掌), 靜截, 靜罡, 靜陣; 靜畢 graduation (實戰100萬 基內50 拆招80), 靜功 武林大會. — jyfreeonline; index_jy2-12, 2-29-3.
- [confirmed] Other NPCs: 丁敏君, 周芷若, 紀曉芙 (outside), 靜玄 (禪定 → 內力), 靜照 (易理/讀書, 守宮砂 trial), 貝錦丁/貝綿丁/貝綿儀 (踏牆; keep one canonical id), 蓮香 (禪修, fixed hours, lowers 殺氣), 靜觀, 金小妹, 劍痴, 農夫, 峨嵋信差 (2001-12-07). — mengpai/3, gonglue/2, tishi.
- [confirmed] Arts: 基本 峨嵋心法(內), 峨嵋身法(輕), 飄雪穿雲掌(掌), 金頂綿掌(掌), 迴風拂柳劍(劍); 特殊 峨嵋九陽功(內), 滅劍(劍), 絕劍(劍), 佛光普照掌(掌). 四象掌 appears in the 17173 全武功表 峨嵋 block (likely 1.0A-era; its 羅煞劍 team gate is later). **峨嵋劍法 is not a real 峨嵋 art** (GDD placeholder; replace with 迴風拂柳劍). No 刀/棍/鞭/暗/醫/毒. — mengpai/3; gaoshou/13.
- [confirmed] Shapes: 迴風拂柳劍 小十字; 滅劍 大十字; 飄雪穿雲掌, 金頂綿掌 直線. Later: 金頂 大十字 from 70, 滅劍 半米字 60–90, 絕劍/四象/截手 半米字, 佛光/天罡指 小十字. — gaoshou/32; blog.
- [confirmed] 2001 gates (player post): 峨嵋九陽功 = 斷惡 50 → 靜照 gives 守宮砂證明 → 滅絕. 滅劍 = 迴風拂柳劍 30 + 九陽 learned + 丁敏君's task (鐵指環) → 滅絕. 守宮砂 chain: 貝綿儀 (峨嵋心法 20) → letter to 靜照 → 殲逞盜賊令 → 斷惡 ≥ 50 → 守宮砂證明. — 2001-08-13; blog.
- [later] Later gates/caps (九陽 cap 80, 斷惡30; 滅劍 90; 絕劍 80 + 紀曉芙 鐵焰令; 佛光 80 + 周芷若/張無忌 letters + 50,000-HP duel; 四象掌 70; 截手九式 60; 天罡指穴法 90; 峨嵋陣法). 佛光 via the real 鐵指環 existed by 2001-12-07 (信差 fix). — blog; tishi.
- [confirmed] Dated sect quests: 孤鴻子師伯的死因 (靜觀, 2001-07-18), 峨嵋的修練 (2001-07-26), 冷凝劍事件 (劍痴, 2001-08-10), 金小妹 烤香魚 (2001-11-29); 替天行道 and 金小妹減肥 are fan-only. — tishi; blog.
- [confirmed] Sect life: 踏牆 (貝綿丁; 氣/神), 易理 (靜照; 精), 禪定 (靜玄; 精/神), 禪修 (蓮香, fixed hours; lowers 殺氣); only 農夫 as a profession; no meal rule. — mengpai/3.
- Open: 招式 names (none; the project's em_jf_1 金頂佛光 is invented); 斷惡 proc numbers; 2001 caps; 善良/邪惡 numeric cut-offs (official expulsion line −299; map 邪惡 to ≤ −300 【待考證】); room layout.

### 恆山派 (hengshan)

- [corrected] Gate 15243,6043 → [635,252], ~740 units north of the 西夏→華山 line (north road). 儀琳 + 華山 英兒 at 14217,5313 「恒山外稻田后方岸边」 = WNW of the gate (not WSW); our hf_paddy/hf_shore rooms are an acceptable abstraction. 江南七怪 stand on snow above (north of) the gate. — 1.0A — gaoshou/17, 23.
- [later] Free-version scenes 2100 (恆山山道 / 山下 events; 定靜 at 恆山山道 470,519), 2101 (main interior, 儀和), 2102 (後山; 藥箱 in the 第一間屋), 2104 (二師姐, 儀悲); 恆山右上破廟. 無色庵/白雲庵/見性峰/懸空寺 have **no game evidence**. — jyfreeonline henshan.
- [confirmed] Join (17173): 中立以上, 無門派, 無PK值, 女. Free/懷舊: 善惡 ≥ 0, 女 (男性需特殊條件), 不得結婚 (還俗 event). Male route is blog-only. — 1.0A / later — mengpai/7; jyfree jy1_1_9.
- [confirmed] Entry rules: disciples without breaches enter; non-disciples neutral+ with no PK enter freely; below neutral or with PK → high chance of a fight on entry; fighting the 恆山知客弟子 → 恆山公敵; betrayal → 公敵, never rejoin; leaving: no conditions. — mengpai/7.
- [confirmed] Sect skill 慈悲 → 反擊: 「機率在被敵攻擊時反擊, 會耗精與內力」 (official, late page). gaoshou/16 (early design copy): stronger healing, small heal on companions, enough 慈悲 lets you make 天香斷續膏, weakness: 拆招卸力 rises slowly. Default 反擊; conflict 【待考證】. — index_jy1-4-11; gaoshou/16.
- [confirmed] Every 恆山 art is gated by the sect stat (pages say 佛法值, a 少林 copy-paste; it means 慈悲). Training: 禪悟 (佛道心經 from 于嫂, click 于嫂, 扣神), 早晚課 (儀質, two specific 時辰, more 慈悲). Killing good NPCs lowers 慈悲. — mengpai/7, gonglue/7.
- [confirmed] 門規 (17173): no casual PK, 善惡 not too low, 殺氣 not too high, no harming fellow disciples, no killing good NPCs. Offences (犯戒): 善惡過低, 有PK玩家且殺了人, 同門自殘, 殺氣超高 → cannot enter 恆山, cannot learn, NPCs won't trigger, until you repent. Pre-11-15: 同門相殘 → expulsion + 公敵. Official later: 善惡 < 0, PK > 1, 同門相殘; enforcer 「無（因為進不了恆山）」. The 2001-08 list adds 殺氣 > 3000. — mengpai/7; index_jy1-4-2, 1-4-3; news 141.
- [confirmed] Masters 1.0A (17173): 恆山派弟子 儀和 (恆山心法, 恆山身法, 恆山劍法); 定逸師太 (天長掌法); unnamed 恆山派掌門 (萬法劍法 = typo for 萬花劍法; the 掌門 is likely 定閒師太 【待考證】). Others: 定靜師太 (修定 → 基本內功; 精/神), 儀文 (縱山 → 恆山身法 + 基本輕功; 精), 儀真 (識藥: hold 恆山醫書 from 儀真, click 儀真 in three specific 時辰 → +醫術, 扣神), 于嫂 (慈悲), 儀質 (早晚課), 何月 (firewood), 程薇 (farming). — mengpai/7, gonglue/7.
- [later] Free roster: 儀和 also 恆山醫術; 大師姐 天長; 代理掌門人 萬花; 二師姐 北嶽劍訣; 儀悲 慈悲療法; 儀心 慈悲心法; 儀令 劍陣; 儀清 (letter for 天長; 還俗); 魔除 萬花 70–80. — jyfree; blog.
- [confirmed] Arts 1.0A: 基本 恆山心法(內), 恆山身法(輕), 恆山劍法(劍); 特殊 天長掌法(掌), 萬花劍法(劍). 恆山醫法 is in the client dump and the gaoshou/32 range table (小十字) but not on mengpai/7's learnable list → it exists, teacher unknown (default 儀和 【待考證】). 北嶽劍訣, 慈悲療法, 慈悲心法, 恆山劍陣 are later. No 招式 anywhere. — mengpai/7; gaoshou/13, 32.
- [confirmed] Shapes: 恆山劍法 and 醫法 小十字 (5 cells). — gaoshou/32.
- [later] Free numbers: 恆山心法 cap 100 (0.6+10); 恆山身法 needs 恆心10 慈悲10 (0.82+2); 恆山劍法 慈悲5 (0.83+3, "as strong as 華山劍法"); 天長掌法 恆心30 慈悲30 悟20 + 儀清 letter, 定逸 (0.86+1, strong defence, small cross); 萬花劍法 cap 80, 恆心50 恆劍50 慈悲50 (master raise: 慈悲60 定力24 善惡≥500 + 定靜 battle, 6 enemies × 2000 HP; 1.48+5000); 基本醫術 cap 120 (0.75+2); 慈悲療法 cure ladder (10 逼毒+醉 … 120 昏迷/無力). — blog.
- [corrected] 恆山 is the medicine sect. Player-sect founding table (2002+): 醫 +20, 毒 −50, and −30 only for **other sects'** arts (恆山's own arts unaffected). 醫 ≥ 30 = 「正常使用恆山醫法」 (below 30 = same effect as a non-恆山 user). — index_jy2-29-5.
- [confirmed] Sect life (17173): 練功 sparring, 縱山 (儀文; 精), 修定 (定靜; 精/神), 識藥, 禪悟, 早晚課. Only basic skills can be trained inside the mountain. No meal rule. Chore NPCs 何月 (firewood), 程薇 (poor harvest). — mengpai/7; gonglue/7.
- [later] Graduation 實戰100萬 基內50 拆招50 基本醫術40 (掌管畢業師姐 / 畢業後回派師兄); 三定 chain, 定性劍, 古玉戒, 還俗 event. — index_jy2-29-3; blog.
- Open: 反擊 vs heal proc; per-art 慈悲 thresholds; teacher of 萬花 (掌門 = 定閒?) and of 恆山醫法; 招式; caps; interior room names; who you repent to; gate-guard strength.

### 全真教 (quanzhen; 終南山 重陽宮)

- [confirmed] NPC sect in 1.0A: a 2001-09-03 HP list covers every 全真 NPC; a 2001-08-26 article calls 全真 (map 4300) a crowded training spot; quests dated 2001-08-31, 11-29, 12-07. — 17173 2001-09-03 n362_279137; 2001-08-26 n355_272026; tishi.
- [corrected] Gate: primary reading 17630,11000 (secondary 17407,10987) → use **[735,458]**; 知客道長 at wild 17600,11200 (later guide). — gaoshou/17; steamxo 2020/07/31/1130525.
- [corrected] 古墓 is in 終南山後山 (official beginner walkthrough, twice). The 大理 → 少林山腳 escort route passes 終南山 (players join 古墓 via 孫婆婆 on the way). 16845,10507 「終南山左上方河邊」 is **陳大寶** (幫助陳大寶, 2001-07-26), not 劍隱. — index_jy2-6-3; gonglue/6; gaoshou/23.
- [corrected] Rooms: 2001 client map id 4300 = 全真 (a bot training spot inside). Later guide: 終南山門外 (知客道長) → right → first fork left into **三清殿** (丘處機 445,520; 馬鈺 340,550; 王處一 235,660; 孫不二 190,765; 劉處玄 60,615; 郝大通 150,635; 祈志誠 580,430 teaches basics); 重陽宮 (掌藥大師兄 280,805); 尹志平 106,670 and 養生隱士 150,830 (paid) on 終南山; 掌園道長 後山 1356,1134; 鹿清篤 1335,260 hands out meals. jb136: 讀書 in 「丘初級屋子」 (cap 61 → 71 after an update). — steamxo; jb136.
- [confirmed] Join (early 17173): 善惡不低於初始數值, 無門派, 男性, not 全真/古墓公敵, 未婚; non-members join via the 知客弟子; fighting him → 全真公敵; disciples come and go freely. Later: 善惡 ≥ 0, PK < 10, mountain entry only for 善惡 ≥ +300. 2001-08-08: 「全真那個看門老頭」 refused evil players. Female routes are 免費版 and conflict. — mengpai/6; orientaldragon node/47; 2001-08-08 n6_922508.
- [confirmed] Sect skill 養生 → 休息: 「機率回復氣與內力, 會耗精與神」. Taught by 清和道人 尹志平; do not train with 內功 at the same time (走火入魔); macros trained it 「到後山練養生處」. Below a skill's 養生 limit you can use but not train it. 飲酒 and entering 古墓 lower 養生. — index_jy2-25; mengpai/6; jb63.
- [confirmed] Traits (2002, mainland 成都6服): 陣法威力最強, 內力療傷效果較強, 學習外功速度最緩慢, hardest sect to level. — 2002-05-07 n985_901436.
- [corrected] 門規 early (mengpai/6): 1 善惡不得低於中立 2 殺氣不得太重 3 不得進入古墓 4 不得有PK值 5 不能結婚 (**no exception** in the early text) 6 不能飲酒. Penalties: 飲酒/古墓 → 減低養生; 停止傳功 for low 善惡, high 殺氣, PK, low 養生; 逐出 for 善惡過低, 殺氣過高, PK值過多 (= 叛教: 本門武功減半, 公敵). 2001-08 叛派 list: 善惡 < −299, PK > 10, 同門相殘, 結婚. Official later: 善惡 < −399, killing > 10 古墓 disciples, PK > 10, 同門相殘, unauthorised marriage, 公敵; enforcers = 全真七子. — mengpai/6; news 141; index_jy2-22.
- [confirmed] Masters: the 全真七子 each take their own disciples, and you learn only from your own master. All seven teach 全真心法, 金雁功, 全真劍法, 天罡北斗陣, 昊天掌. Seventh skill: 馬鈺 + 劉處玄 → 先天氣功; 王處一 + 譚處端 + 孫不二 → 同歸劍法; 丘處機 + 郝大通 → 三花聚頂掌. — mengpai/6.
- [confirmed] 2001-09-03 HP roster: 丘處機 12500, 馬鈺 12000, 譚處端 11500, 王處一 11001, 劉處玄 11000, 郝大通 10500, 孫不二 9500, 尹志平 9500, 趙志敬 9500, 王志坦 9000, 李志常 9000, 崔志方 8500, 申志凡 8500, 鹿清篤 8000, 姬清虛 7200, 皮清玄 7000, 夏志誠 5200, 艾清劫/江清督/郝清諸/知客道長 5000, 陳志溢/賈清白/王清豐 4000, 王志明/朱清廷 3000, 方清嵩 2500. Third-generation 趙志敬, 李志常, 王志坦, 崔志方, 申志凡 spar for 外功 (精氣神; gives 殺氣, 實戰, 潛能). — 2001-09-03.
- [corrected] 夏志誠 teaches 讀書識字; a 後山 洞口道士 at 子時 randomly lets you see 王重陽 (portrait +100 基本內功 熟練, 精/神); a 後山山洞 基本內功 teacher (rumoured to 100). — jb136, jb116.
- [refuted as stated] The early page has tier headings but no skill mapping. Later official: 基本 全真心法/全真劍法/昊天掌/金雁功; 進階 先天氣功/同歸劍法/三花聚頂掌/天罡北斗陣; 特殊 重陽劍法. Categories (official 自宮 list): 內 全真心法, 先天氣功; 劍 全真劍法, 同歸劍法, 重陽劍法; 拳 空明拳; 輕 金雁功; 搏 昊天掌, 三花聚頂掌; 陣 天罡北斗陣. — index_jy2-18.
- [confirmed] 2002 requirements (mainland): 同歸 全心60 全劍50 養生70 基內50 基劍40 (係數1); 三花 昊天70 全心70 基內60 搏擊60 (1.25); 先天氣功 全心90 養生100 基內80; 重陽劍法 全心70 基劍60 基內60 養生80 (2.25); 空明拳 昊天70 搏擊80 全心80 養生90 福32 善1000 (2); 天罡 心法80 全劍70 養生90 基內70 基劍60. Coefficients 全心 0.8, 全劍 0.74, 昊天 0.95. 金雁功 「增加戰鬥中的移動距離」. Per-master caps (全劍/昊天/三花/同歸/天罡/先天): 丘 100/80/80/80/80/–; 馬 80/80/–/70/80/80; 譚 80/80/–/90/80/–; 劉 80/70/–/–/80/70. — 2002-05-07.
- [unverifiable] Later shapes: 全真劍法 小十字, 昊天掌 大十字 (faster), 同歸/三花 半米字. — blog.
- [partially confirmed] 天罡北斗陣: 7 male party members; leader 佈陣, members stand on dots and attack with 特 (劍 + 全真劍法); very large range, defence up; if any member dies the formation breaks. — blog; mengpai/6.
- [corrected] 重陽劍法 (七星七絕劍) chain by 2002: 百花谷 老頭 to 20 → self to 30 → 3 瓶狀元紅 to 漱流隱士 (「左下地圖的雪山右下方，兩面靠水」) to 40 → 雲遊道士 (南帝隱所, needs 拂塵) to 60. 空明拳 teacher conflict (周伯通 vs 王志謹). 一劍化三清, 先天功, 天罡指 are later. — 2002-05-07; blog; steamxo.
- [confirmed] 2001 quests: 丘處機與江南七怪; 王處一之傷 (four herbs); 楊康與郭靖 / 尋找楊康 (2001-08-31); 全真教與活死人墓之爭; 尹志平思慕小龍女; 賊擾終南山; 黑風雙煞; 幫孫不二跑腿; 江清睿的煩惱 (farming); 終南山在哪裡? (浪子程風); 2001-11-29 買工具, 鹿清篤買菜, 陳志益採礦 (ore → probably money); 2001-12-07 鹿清篤的打工事件, 全真切磋事件 (後山 師兄 at 子時, needs a 切磋證). — gonglue/6, tishi, jb125.
- [corrected] 小教 (early) = fight your master, 「過招三回合不敗」 (no death penalty) → 大教資格證; 大教 = PK tournament, top 7 get 天權/玉衡/天璇/天樞/天璣/開陽/搖光真人. Later spelled 小較/大較. — mengpai/6; orientaldragon.
- [confirmed] Sect life: 重陽宮 food at 卯/午/酉, 全真弟子 only, one portion each (鹿清篤); 內功 by meditating anywhere, bonus in the 後山 閉關山洞; 輕功 from your master (精/神); 外功 by sparring the third generation. — mengpai/6; steamxo.
- [confirmed] Evil farming: 善惡 < −500 → 全真弟子 attack inside (17 disciples); the gatekeeper refuses evil players, so a party member must bring them in; 「血洗全真」 lowers 善惡. 2002 benchmark: ~10萬 實戰 / 特武 40. — 2001-08-08; jb22.
- [confirmed] Betrayal: 全真教公敵, random 全真 disciple fights, never rejoin, all 終南山 NPCs fight you; expulsion halves 本門武功 (later: everything incl. basics, sect skill → 0). Graduation (later): 實戰100萬 基內50 拆招50 全真心法50 (掌畢業師兄 / 畢業後回派師兄). — mengpai/6; index_jy2-6-7, 2-29-3.
- Open: 養生 thresholds per level; 招式; 1.0A per-master caps; 1.0A 門規 numbers; whether the +300 mountain-entry rule existed in 2001; 2001 room layout; female entry; 養生 proc numbers; the meaning of 「丘初級」.

### 古墓派 (gumu)

- [confirmed] Primary source: the official pre-launch page index_jy2-4e.asp archived 2001-06-10 (Big5; ends 「以上文字皆為開發中資料」). Sibling pages 4a 少林, 4b 武當, 4c 峨嵋, 4d 血刀, 4f 全真, 4g 恆山; no 華山/星宿/靈鷲/丐幫 page in 2001-06. 17173 mengpai/5 is a simplified copy. — web.archive.org/web/20010610100744/http://www.chinesegamer.net:80/jinyong/index_jy2-4e.asp.
- [confirmed] Location: 終南山活死人墓 (same mountain as 全真); a 公敵 arriving at 終南山活死人墓 fights every 古墓 NPC; 離山條件: disciples may not leave 終南山 without a quest (enforcement unknown — owner decision); 「古墓派弟子，終其一生多都必須在古墓裡」 (林朝英's rule). Later entrance 野外 17990,10918 → ~[750,455] just east of the 終南 gate; 孫婆婆 at 古墓 242,392; later 小龍女 at 絕情谷 (1.0B+). — 2001 page; steamxo 1130523.
- [confirmed] Map: the interior is a maze; disciples get a guide (「迷宮內可享有專人帶路」); sectless players enter to apprentice via 孫婆婆; fighting 孫婆婆 → 古墓派公敵; forcing entry without 孫婆婆 → constant high-rate battles but **not** 公敵. 寒玉床 room (2001); 小梅's room (c.2002, 清心 clicks); 農田 pair practice (free). Cave danger (2001-07-05): many monsters, **no fleeing**, 洪凌波/李莫愁 may join fights; 2002 guide: 65000–120000 實戰 「古墓派 4个35点」 = 4 enemies, 35 points per fight. — 2001 page; 17173 2001-07-05 n501_417412; gaoshou/31.
- [confirmed] Join (2001 official): 善惡 中立, 無門派, 女性, 未婚, not 古墓公敵, 守宮砂標記不得消失; via 孫婆婆; only one entry master. 17173 2001-07-08: 善惡 between 平善 and 平惡, 女性, 拜見古墓派掌門; refused if outside the band or male. −299~299 is the later numeric band. Male evidence (GS1 「兩個師兄」) is ambiguous (人妖 = male players on female characters). The male route (全真公敵 + 全真心法30 …) is 免費版. — 2001 page; n32_948265; jb102.
- [confirmed] 守宮砂: required to join and to stay; losing it or marrying → 逐出師門. How it is gained/lost is undocumented. — 2001 page.
- [confirmed] Sect skill 清心: taught by 古墓派掌門人 or 小龍女, disciples only, not trained together with 內功, costs 精/神; gates all arts (below the limit: usable, not trainable); 實戰經驗 also gates further training; repeat violations can expel. Official proc table: 痲痺 「機率使敵痲痺, 會耗精與神」. The 2001-07-05 "dodge" line is pre-launch copy (use 痲痺; dodge 【待考證】). — 2001 page; index_jy2-25.
- [confirmed] 門規 (2001 official): 善惡 中立, 不能結婚, 守宮砂不得消失. 停止傳功 for 善惡 outside neutral, PK過高, 殺氣過重, 清心不足. 逐出 for 守宮砂消失 / marriage = 叛師 (本門武功減半, 公敵). 2001-08-16 叛派: 善惡 < −500, 殺氣 > 2000, 結婚 (news 141 also PK > 3). Later official jail list: PK > 10, 殺氣 > 10000, enforcer 古墓派掌門. — 2001 page; n315_231423; news 141; index_jy2-22.
- [confirmed] Masters (2001): 古墓派掌門人 (generic title, no personal name) and 小龍女 teach; some disciples help practise; the disciple beside 寒玉床 teaches its use; 孫婆婆 teaches the rules and is the gatekeeper. 內功 古墓心法, 玉女心經 (掌門/小龍女); 外功 天羅地網掌法, 玉女劍法, 全真劍法, 美女拳法 (掌門/小龍女), 玉女素心劍法, 銀索金鈴 (小龍女 only); 輕功 天羅地網身法, 迎風拂柳步 (掌門/小龍女). 九陰真經 is listed 進階 but has **no teacher** (self-study/quest). 小仙 = 武林大會 NPC (late). — 2001 page; index_jy2-12.
- [corrected] 2001 tiers: 基本 古墓心法, 玉女劍法, 全真劍法, 天羅地網勢身法, 美女拳法; 進階 玉女心經, 天羅地網勢掌法, 迎風拂柳步, 九陰真經; 特殊 玉女素心劍法, 銀索金鈴. No 玉蜂針/冰魄銀針 in 2001 (the ylib 11:05 post is a fan wishlist; the 11:08 letter confirms 打坐 and the 寒玉床 精氣神 drain). There is no art named 「古墓輕功」. The 17173 全武功表 listing (with 丐幫/青城 arts) is 1.0B or later. — 2001 page; ylib secttalk no=2031; gaoshou/13.
- [later] Free table: 玉女劍法 1.12 (early fan 0.96 — GDD uses 0.96), 美女拳 0.86+2, 天羅地網勢 0.84+2, 迎風拂柳步 cap 80, 玉蜂針, 銀索金鈴 (鞭; 清心70; 米字 36 cells), 天羅地網掌 (清心70; strong defence), 美女劍法, 玉女心經 2.95+1000 (pair practice in 農田), 九陰真經 4.5 (福緣30 hidden wall). 李莫愁 evil line (拂塵功, 赤煉神掌, 三無三不手, 冰魄銀針) — exclude. — blog; qqnjy.
- [confirmed] Shapes: 玉女劍法 小十字 (5 cells at a target cell); 美女拳法 直線 (one direction; reach ~4 【待考證】). 玉女素心劍法 is a 合體技 (free: male 全真 + female 古墓 with 默契, same battle, within 3 s) — with 2001 female-only 古墓 and male-only 全真, a cross-sect pair is the only possible pairing. — gaoshou/32; blog.
- [confirmed] Training (2001): meditate (打坐) anywhere for 內功; the 寒玉床 room gives a bonus but 「精氣神會大幅度下降」; 輕功 costs 精/神; 外功 by 對招 with 古墓 disciples (精氣神, gives 殺氣/實戰/潛能). Life skills are open with the right tool. — 2001 page.
- [confirmed] Food: 「卯時、午時、酉時會發放食物，**任何人**都可以…向特定之弟子拿取」. Free: 蓉兒; outsiders get a poisoned bun. — 2001 page; blog.
- [later] Chores/minigames (採水果, 捉蜜蜂, 刺字, 打魚, 抓麻雀), quests (打倒郝大通, 擊退李莫愁, 掌門退位 …), graduation (實戰100萬 基內50 拆招50 閃躲50, 古墓派掌門人). 17173 c.2001–02 quest titles: 赤煉仙子李莫愁, 全真教與活死人墓之爭, 賊擾終南山, 絕情谷主公孫止. — blog; gonglue/5; index_jy2-29-3.
- [confirmed] Weapon affinity (player-sect table, 2003): 毒 −30, 醫 −50, 刀/棍/刺/槍 −30, 劍/拳/鞭/暗 0. — index_jy2-29-5.
- Open: 招式 (none; novel names must be 【設計提案】); per-art 清心 thresholds; 1.0A caps; proc numbers; 守宮砂 mechanics; numeric 中立 band (the 2001 betrayal line < −500 differs from the 停止傳功 band); maze layout and monster names; 九陰真經 availability; whether 全真劍法 is the shared skill; 離山 enforcement.

### 靈鷲宮 (lingjiu)

- [corrected] Present and joinable by 2001-07-08 (the 「拜入十大名門派的基本條件」 article gives conditions for 5 sects: 華山, 武當, 古墓, 星宿, 靈鷲 — the body does not list 10). Conditions: 善惡 between 平善 and 平惡, 女性, 拜見天山童姥. The 2001-08-16 叛派 list includes 靈鷲 (only 同門相殘). 程清霜的煩惱 was added in the 2001-08-17 update. Resolves GDD 附錄B #1 as "present and joinable by 2001-07-08". — n32_948265; tishi; n315_231423.
- [confirmed] **No 1.0 coordinate**: absent from the 2001-07-06 coordinate list (as are 古墓 and 京城) and from the 17173 中原地圖. The early 17173 intro (xinshou_2) puts 靈鷲宮 in the **邊疆** group with 昆侖山域, 光明頂, 星宿海 → far west/north-west near 西夏 (6700,2700) and 星宿海 (3000,4600). In 1.0 only 西夏 and 大理 were birth towns, so 西夏 is the natural nearest town. — gaoshou/17; xinshou_2; yinan/35.
- [confirmed] Scenes (official, cumulative list): 靈鷲宮室外景, 室內景1/2/3. Later NPC coords (ali213, 2004-11 metadata): 陳季妃 (456,296 求收留), 天山童姥 (2364,764), 周婆婆 鸞天部, 石嫂 朱天部 (medicine), 程清霜 鈞天部, 定性師姐 (silver), 竹劍, 吳梅, 梅劍, 菊劍, 蘭劍 (定坐), 小彩. — jyfree jy1_2; ali213 465607.
- [confirmed] Entry: 「靈鷲宮不得進入參觀」 (no visitors); 「本門弟子無門禁」. Join (official): 善惡 −299…299, 女 (「男性無法拜入」 on jyfree), not 靈鷲公敵, PK ≤ 3 (the PK clause is absent from the 2001-07-08 article); join by talking to 天山童姥. No marital/attribute/test requirement. No male route in 1.0 (only a client-hack bug). — index_jy1-1-1-10; jyfree jy1_1_4; n32_948265.
- [confirmed] Sect skill 定性 → 混亂: 「機率使敵混亂, 會耗精與神」; 定性 practice costs 神 (扣神); gates advanced arts; **drops when 殺氣 is too high**. 1.0-era event teaser: 蘭劍 teaches 定性 (「幸好還有蘭劍姊姊會教」); 竹劍 and the paid 定性師姐 are later. Early text: 定性 raises medicine effect; sect arts give bonus defence and +movement; vs male opponents attack up / defence down. 2003: 靈鷲/古墓 players walk faster on the map. — index_jy2-25; index_jy1-1-1-10; gaoshou/16; 鐵心網 faq.
- [confirmed] 門規: keep 善惡 neutral; 定性 not too low; no 同門相殘 (players and NPCs); PK ≤ 3. Violations: 善惡 outside ±299 → cannot learn; 同門相殘. Punishment: 童姥 refuses to teach until fixed. Enforcer: 無. Betrayal: 「以後將不得重新加入」. — index_jy2-22; index_jy1-1-1-10.
- [corrected] Masters 1.0: **only 天山童姥 teaches martial arts** (「傳功只有天山童姥一個人」); 逍遙派 arts come from events. Teasers: 蘭劍 teaches 定性 (定坐修內力 is phrased as a question), 菊劍 teaches 輕功 (探路), 程清霜 encourages 紡織 and gives items, 符敏儀 has a chill. — index_jy1-1-1-10; index_jy3-1-10.
- [later] Roster 周婆婆 (basics), 蘭劍 八荒 消點 40→70, 小彩 (逍遙無極陣, 武林大會), 李秋水 (白虹掌, 西夏皇宮), 逍遙子/無涯子 (北冥), 竹劍, 梅劍, 石嫂, 穆雯青 (graduation 實戰100萬 基內50 拆招50 定性50). GDD's current 靈鷲 table is this later roster. — blog; ali213; index_jy2-29-3.
- [confirmed] Arts (official): 基本 逍遙心法, 月影舞步, 天山折梅手, 天羽奇劍, 如意刀法; 進階 八荒六合唯我獨尊功, 天山六陽掌 (the undated a6 page lists 六陽 as 基本 and 八荒/小無相功/北冥神功/凌波微步 as event 特殊). Gated by 定性; dropping below a threshold freezes training. 習武: learn in the palace and 對招 with palace NPCs. — index_jy1-1-1-10; a6.
- [later] Free numbers: 逍遙心法 cap 80 (0.84+1); 月影舞步 80 (jumps obstacles, +1 tile per 20 lv); 天羽奇劍 100 (逍遙10 定性20, 半米字, fast); 如意刀法 100 (逍遙10 定性10, line); 天山折梅手 cap 80 then self-train (逍遙20 定性20, small cross, **hits inflict 封穴**, 系數 1.508; GDD uses 1.25); 天山六陽掌 (折梅90 定性50; 2.27); 八荒 (逍遙80 門技50; quest 服侍童姥 → 無涯子 → 虛竹 → 童姥). — blog.
- [confirmed] Shapes (17173 2001): 折梅 小十字; 天羽 大十字 ±3 (text 共10格; 鐵心網 2003 says line; blog 半米字 → 【待考證】); 如意刀法, 天山六陽掌 直線. — gaoshou/32; 鐵心網 range.
- [confirmed] 生死符 → cured by 天山六陽掌 (「折梅100級以上的人才學的到」); detailed 生死符 mechanics and 寒冰生死符 (京城) are later. — gaoshou/28; blog.
- [confirmed] Sect skills (official): 定坐 (內功, 精), 生死打 (外功 on palace NPCs, 精), 探路 (輕功, 精), 定性 (神), 裁縫 (神). Early events: 學習定性 (蘭劍), 計整知穴, 好心的程清霜, 符敏儀事件, 定坐修內力, 探路學輕功. 程清霜的煩惱 (2001-08-18 guide): disciples only, 戌時1–6刻, carry medicine to 霍島主 in 大理 (白勇 near the meat stall takes it), reward 千年人參. No meal mechanic. — index_jy1-1-1-10; index_jy3-1-10; 17173 2001-08-18.
- [unverifiable] 機關人 (等級 中, HP 3000, at 靈鷲, suggested level 27) — undated table. — gaoshou/25.
- Open: gate coordinate (proposal: closed fork NW of 西夏 toward 天山 【待考證】); room→NPC layout; per-art 定性 thresholds; 六陽掌 tier and prerequisite (折梅90 vs 100); availability of the 特殊 arts; launch 定性 effect; the PK ≤ 3 clause at launch.

### 星宿派 (xingxiu; 星宿海)

- [confirmed] Sect skill 諂媚 → 分心: 「機率使敵攻擊力下降, 會耗精與神」; no numbers. A 2002 mainland player: procs only mattered after 光明頂 and 星宿's was barely noticeable; 武當 俠義 briefly made 星宿 poison almost never land. Keep defaults small. — index_jy2-25; 17173 2002-02-22 n445_361244.
- [confirmed] 諂媚 gates learning (「學習新武功會依情況要求諂媚與毒術到達一定數值」); below a limit an art is usable but not trainable. High 殺氣 lowers 諂媚 (rate unknown). — Wayback 2004 mengpai/8; 17173 2018 repost 200028671.
- [confirmed] 門規: 17173 「1. 不能為善 2. 需時時諂媚」; official 「若善惡值大於 −100，則會無法學習武功」, enforcer 無; 犯戒: 丁春秋 stops teaching until 善惡 returns to 中立以下 — the launch text treats 「大於−100」 and 「中立以下」 as the same boundary. No jail, no expulsion for this. 2001-08 叛派: issuing a PK command on 丁春秋. — index_jy2-22; mengpai/8; news 141.
- [corrected] Join: 2004 text 善惡值為惡, 無門派, 未被星宿派做上叛徒標記; 2018 text 善惡值中立以下. **Use 善惡 ≤ −100** for 1.0 (−299 is the later ±299 neutral band). 出派條件 無特別限制; a betrayer → 星宿公敵, never rejoins. Both genders (懷舊 jyhj; not a 2001 source). No attribute/PK/marriage/test. The 採花子 → 摘星子 join flow is 2020 only. — mengpai/8; jyhj.
- [confirmed] Gate 3408,4608 → [142,192], SW of 西夏 (7296,2966). — gaoshou/17.
- [partially confirmed] Scenes (official list, not re-fetched): 星宿海外景 (wooden pier and stilt houses over water, boat landing, a huge sea-serpent sculpture), 大殿 (red carpet, ladders in the corners), 右房, 左房, 中房一樓, 中房二樓. Rejoin point verified: 「星宿外面廣場到大廳的進出點」. — jyfree jy1_2; index_jy2-29-3.
- [confirmed] Masters 1.0: 「此門派功夫由丁春秋一人傳授」. The 2004 page lists 8 special skills (星宿心法, 放毒法, 星宿身法, 化功大法, 抽髓掌, 三陰蜈蚣爪, 天山杖法, 飛星術); the earlier 2018 launch-template text lists **only 6** (no 化功大法, no 飛星術) → core 1.0 set = 6; 化功/飛星 【待考證】. 星宿逍遙掌 is a 成名技 by 2004-09. NPC names 獅吼子, 出塵子, 疏斗子, 燦極子 exist (quest list undated, earliest capture 2004). — mengpai/8; 2018 repost; gonglue/8.
- [later] Free table: 星宿心法 100 (0.5+10); 星宿身法 100 (0.83+1); 抽髓掌 100 (心法10 諂媚10 基毒10; 0.87+3); 放毒法 毒 cap 120 (心法10 諂媚15 基毒20; 0.96+2); 三陰蜈蚣爪 80 (心法20/30 諂媚30 基毒40; 1.431+5000; line at 1–39, large cross from 40); 天山杖法 棍 100 (0.9+3; 半米字); 飛星術 暗 (心法50 門技40 基毒50 善惡−1000; self to 15 then a master); 化功大法 (everything 100). Bahamut 2004-09: 抽髓掌 入門 0.870, 天山杖法 進階 0.900, 三陰蜈蚣爪 上乘 1.43+5000, 逍遙掌 成名技 1.9+5000, 飛星術 暗器 0.590. — blog; Bahamut G2 bsn=2605 sn=235.
- [confirmed] Launch-era text: 星宿身法 → 閃躲縱躍 proficiency; 放毒法 → 毒術, 暗器; 抽髓掌 → 搏擊格鬥, 毒術; 三陰/天山杖 → 毒術. 「武功由於每招均需以毒為基礎，所以每次的出招也能使敵人有機率中毒」 → **every 星宿 sect attack can poison** (separate from the 毒 command). The 2018 text labels these 基本武功. — 2018 repost.
- [confirmed] 放毒 delivery (2002): 「星宿的都會走過來，係你面前出手…光明正大地放毒，你看見可以避開」 → short range, visible, avoidable. — 2002-02-22.
- [corrected] The 毒-attribute tier table (一般毒 11 … 星宿專有毒 51, 毒 +20) is the **player-sect founding** table (「此表只與玩家新創立門派有關，與原NPC門派無關」), 2002–2003. Do not use it for 1.0 numbers. — index_jy2-29-5.
- [corrected] Sect job skills: 煉毒 (needs a 小木鼎 + a 特定狀況; raises 毒術; 精), 馬屁經 (talk to specific NPCs; raises 諂媚; 神), 試武 (innocent villagers as targets; affects 善惡; 精), 步法 (find the footwork disciple; 輕功; 精). Quests: 馬屁研習 (獅吼子), 邪惡的練毒 (出塵子), 狩獵的技巧 (疏斗子), 毒門暗器 (燦極子) — undated. — mengpai/8; 2018; gonglue/8.
- [confirmed] 七毒 quest (楚禮子, 2001-08-18 mainland post): 赤蝎 1991,713; 青蛇 5990,17288; 彩蛛 16608,5256; 蝎子 2634,5600; 青蛇 + 黑蛛 18690,10132; 蜈蚣 6090,774; 蟾蜍 9087,573. ~~The later 咸楓子 set is different — do not merge.~~ **[corrected, M3b]** 咸楓子（出外抓毒物）的毒物點與這七點有 4／5 吻合 → 兩條任務共用一組大地圖毒物點（M3B_PLAN O21；見本檔「未做的 2001 事件」）。 — 17173 2001-08-18 n862_778664.
- [corrected] Betrayal example (jb170, post-京城 so late 2001+): 抽髓掌 20 → 10 after 叛教; a betrayer keeps the art and can still 自修 it. — gaoshou/jb170, jb5.
- [confirmed] Graduation (later): 實戰100萬 基內50 拆招50 **基本毒術80**; 畢業子; rejoin by walking the plaza-to-hall exit. — index_jy2-29-3.
- [later] 飛星術 traitor quest, 化功大法 / 星宿逍遙掌 quests, the poison-crafting table, 絕頂化功術, 毒龍戒, 武侯至尊爪. — blog; qqnjy.
- Open: 招式 names; 諂媚 proc numbers; per-skill 諂媚 thresholds; 1.0 poison numbers (success, level, damage, duration); 煉毒 mechanics; room layout; the route 西夏 → 星宿海 (currently closed past ws_dunes); jail (none: enforcer 無).

### 血刀門 (xuedao)

- [confirmed] Open by July 2001 (2002-05-19 retrospective 「自從七月進入血刀以來」): 血刀刀法 was very strong and had 格 (parry); an August 2001 patch weakened it and removed 格 (difficulty not lowered); the same patch raised 滅仙掌 to cap 100 (so the 2001 cap was lower, value unknown); a later patch restored 格, gave 血海魔功 內力 attack and opened 血魔刀法. — 17173 2002-05-19 n715_631737.
- [confirmed] Sect skill 威嚇 → 重擊: 「機率加強攻擊, 會耗精與內力」 (= HEAVY). Intro text: trained by the 恐嚇 command on NPCs (lowers 善惡, costs much 精/神); 威嚇 is also a prerequisite for 血海魔功 (門派技能60); the intro and a 2002 player also say it weakens enemy attack (unofficial variant 【待考證】). 2003: trained by 比武 at the 血池 (初/中/高). 至尊: the 威嚇師兄 sells it. — index_jy2-25; sina 2011 copies; 2002-10-07; 2003-08-10; jy16.
- [confirmed] 門規 (official): 「嚴禁弒師犯上」; enforcer 「無（因為不會被蓄意開除）」. Intro: no special rules, but 善惡 must stay 邪惡; PK unlimited (system PK rules apply); meat and wine allowed; 先除叛徒，再殺敵人; 善惡 中立以上 → teachers refuse until evil again; attacking 血刀老祖 outside trial time → expelled forever (= betrayal); 血刀公敵: never rejoin, auto-attacked entering 血刀門. 2001-08 叛派: a PK command on 血刀老祖. — index_jy2-22; intro copies; news 141.
- [confirmed] Join: 2003 guide 「入血刀善惡要−99以下」 (male: 善惡 −100); talk to the 血刀弟子 at the gate, then 血刀老祖 (options 滅派 / 拜入); 至尊: < −299. **Use ≤ −100** for 1.0 (no 2001 number exists). Male only (a garbled 2003 female route is not implemented). Players with positive 善惡 are attacked entering 血刀門. — 2003-08-10 n864_780464; jy16.
- [confirmed] Arts: entry 4 (血刀心法 內, 血刀刀法 刀, 滅仙掌 拳掌, 雪遁步行 輕; learned on joining from **勝諦**, no other requirement); 血海魔功 (內, 二階; existed by Jul 2001 but was useless until the 內力 patch; 2002-08 req 實戰300萬 門技60 基內60 基刀60 心法80 刀法80 滅仙掌60 雪遁60 名歷1000 善惡 −900~−1000 + hit 血刀老祖 for 20000+ → lv1, self to 30, then 消點); 血魔刀法 (刀 必殺, 流血; needs 京城 + 雁門關 → **1.0B only**). Only one blade line. — jytzst menpai/12; 2002-03-05, 2002-08-19, 2002-10-07.
- [confirmed] Coefficients: a post-2003 table gives 滅仙掌 0.92+2, 血刀刀法 1.40+8, 血刀心法 0.70+10, 血海魔功 2.75+1000, 雪遁步行 0.82+2. The project's 1.6 (undated blog, different formula) is kept for 1.0A 【待考證】, consistent with "the coefficient stayed high after the nerf". — sina blog_8036153e0100srrj.
- [confirmed] Masters (2002–2003): 血刀老祖 (joining, the 血海 trial, 刀訣), 血海老祖 (血池 behind the hall), 血魔老祖 (血魔 消點), 勝諦 (4 entry arts), 善勇 (門規事件), 苦集, 哀晦, 淨歧, 寶象; 不留 / 不走 graduation (實戰100萬 基內50 拆招50 基本刀法50). 基刀 opened to 120 by 2002-10. — 2002–2003 guides; index_jy2-29-3.
- [confirmed] Training mini-games (2003): 姿式 (血刀心法 < 30, +10), 提縱 (雪遁 < 30; success +10, fail +2), 對招 (心法30 雪遁30), 批紙削腐 (血刀刀法 40–50, +10 per success to 100). No 2001 evidence. — 2003-08-10.
- [corrected] 門規事件 (2002-08): 氣 > 1000, 善惡 < −99 → 善勇 → kill the 血刀叛徒 in the snow NE of 黑龍潭 (via the 雁門關 cave — a later area); kill the 血刀 traitor **first** among 武當/少林/華山 disciples → 名聲+50 歷練+50 雪遁+50, otherwise 名聲+1 歷練+2. — 2002-08-19.
- [corrected] Sect-life quests (2003): 老祖想吃熊肉 (10 熊肉 → 名聲+5 歷練+5 善惡−50); 哀晦 寅時 kidnap 豬哥義's sister in 西夏 (名聲/歷練+10, 善惡−100); 淨歧/淨岐 戌時 buy 五壇美酒 (1000兩); a 鼓勵師兄 粗布道袍 errand (once). No meals or chores like 少林; meat and wine allowed. — 2003-08-10.
- [confirmed] Location: gate 477,12471 → [20,520], far west; rooms (later): 門口 with two 血刀弟子 guards (between 本堂 and 原野), 本堂/大廳 (血刀老祖), 血池 behind the hall (比武 tiers), 密室. Not the same place as 血刀洞迷宮 (8626,2228). — gaoshou/17; 2003-08-10; jy16.
- [refuted] The 2002 memoir of "血刀老祖 attacking players at the foot of 全真" describes a PK player named 血刀老祖, not the NPC. Do not implement. — 2002-09-23.
- [later] 煉獄刀, 封禪台, 千刃斬, 終身弟子, 血魔刀法精要. — 2003+.
- Open: 1.0A join threshold (no 2001 number); 威嚇 proc variant; 1.6 vs 1.40 coefficient; the 2001 滅仙掌 cap and 格 details; 招式; 2001 map layout; whether 血海老祖 existed in 2002-08.

### 戰鬥指令、狀態與門規機制 (cross-sect mechanics)

- [confirmed] Command-bar timeline: news 399 (2002-01-25 決戰光明頂) 「13.戰鬥指令新增內功.輕功.點穴,除去"閃"按鈕」 and 「19.戰鬥中閃躲及防禦改為自動使用,並加入反擊機率判斷」. So **內/輕/穴 are 2002, and the 2001 bar had a 閃 button**. The 17-button list 移攻武特防休裝逃系毒醫暗陣屬內輕穴 (jyfree jy2_3_6) is post-2002. 休 restores 氣/內力 but lowers 精/神; 防 blocks with your art. — news 399; jy2_3_6.
- [confirmed] 毒 opened 2001-08-09 (news 316): poisonous arts used via 特 deal only weapon damage; only 「毒」 inflicts 中毒, and its direct damage is **smaller** than a 特 attack; a poisoned unit loses HP **every time it acts**; after battle the poison keeps flaring **every 2 minutes** while active; an in-battle poison death in PK counts PK值, an after-battle death does not. 2001-10-05: 毒 damage formula nerfed (no formula published). Requirement: 「還沒有學習到毒術武功的人則不能使用」. 基本毒術 = 「此款遊戲中最恐怖也最為強橫的技能」. Non-星宿 poisoners succeed much less often. — news 316, 322; jy2_3_6; jy1-1-5-2; 2002-02-22.
- [later] 制毒, 下毒 into food, 兵器喂毒, 醫術 治毒傷/補氣 (決戰光明頂 2002; 17173 juezhan/3); the ATB gauge slowed by poison depth (news 399 item 17); 逼毒 via 內功 (2002-01-25).
- [confirmed] Poison cures: ordinary poison by any 解毒 medicine; otherwise you lose 氣 until the poison's duration ends; 華陀之書 cures ordinary poison 10 times; 解毒靈藥 / 解毒草 are 2001 quest rewards. Special poisons (生死符, 七心海棠, 三尸腦神丹) are later. — gaoshou/28; jy1-2-4-10.
- [corrected] 醫 existed by 2001-07-20 (news 308: 「[攻,特,醫]…按control按鍵指定目標」); heal yourself or a teammate; needs 醫術. 恆山 醫術 is practised with 恆山醫書 at 儀真 during three set 時辰. No formula. — news 308; jy2_3_6; jy1-1-1-7.
- [confirmed] 2001-08-23 (news 318): medicine usable on own party members in battle; damage applied in two parts; **stats in the attribute lantern change colour when they reach a 叛派 threshold** (a cheap 1.0 warning UI). — news 318.
- [confirmed] 暗 needs a hidden-weapon art **and** 暗器 items. Official 暗器 (可否下毒 / 敵人傷氣 / 重量): 彈弓 可/−2/1, 金錢鏢 可/−8/1, 飛蝗石 可/−12/1, 鐵菱 可/−20/2, 鐵蓮子 可/−35/2 (擊打要穴), 魚鏢 可/−20/2. 基本暗器 pairs well with 點穴. Per-throw consumption is implied, not stated 【待考證】. — jy1-1-4-6; jy1-1-5-2.
- [confirmed] 陣: 陣法 and 合體技 opened 2001-10-05 (神鵰大俠 patch, mandatory update); party only. Old 陣法 rules (jy2-99): while active, only formation arts; the effect is only +atk/+def, scaled by the **average** member 陣法 level; members stand on **fixed stations**; fixed headcount. 少林 金剛伏魔圈 (3, whips, very high 佛法 + 少林心法, 渡厄); 全真 天罡北斗陣 (7, all male, 內力 + 劍法 levels, all 全真七子); 武當 真武七截陣 (7, 宋遠橋; 俠義 gate per the sect page). 基本陣法: 「每次施展也需要相同的人數」. New-system generic formations (松節/背弓/清流/蒼月) are later. — news 322; jy2-99; jy1-2-6-5/6/7.
- [confirmed] 合體技: bond one partner (建立默契); both use the combo art within 3 s; 配合度 → 培養感情 → 默契; decays every 2 days both online; marriage bonus. — jy1-2-6-1.
- [confirmed, 2002] 內 (jy1-4-13, news 395): random attack bonus with the equipped 內功; random defence bonus when hit (more with 防); 逼毒 (level decides how much); 衝穴 (button 衝; chance by 內功 level); 治療內傷 only outside battle; 傳力 to a depleted party member; 點穴 via 穴 (battle only, cured by 衝穴); 內傷 recoil when attacking a stronger 內功. 輕 (news 394): jump over enemies, once per turn when the gauge is full; 原地逃離 like 無敵落跑鞋 but not guaranteed; 基本身法 raises walking speed outside battle (1.0). 穴 / 封穴 / 麻痺 / 混亂 added 2002-01-25; 封穴 = 無法移動, 蓄力變慢, cured by 衝穴 or 心法; governing skill 基本刺術 (「以點、刺、指為主，一般都以點穴、劍氣傷敵」). — 2002-01-25.
- [confirmed] 特 = special attack with the selected art, costs 內力 (2001). — jy2_3_6; news 308.
- [confirmed] Sect-skill combat procs and their costs switched on 2002-01-25 (news 399 item 6; table in news 394/395 and jy1-4-11): 佛法 防禦 精+內力; 俠義 抗技 精+內力; 斷惡 閃避 精+神; 儒風 回復(氣,神) 精+內力; 慈悲 反擊 精+內力; 養生 休息(氣,內力) 精+神; 清心 痲痺 精+神; 定性 混亂 精+神; 諂媚 分心 精+神; 威嚇 重擊 精+內力; 乞討 毒牙 精+神. In 1.0 sect skills gated learning (high-confidence inference).
- [corrected] Status set dated 2001 by an official source (news 316, 2001-08-09): 中毒 綠, 氣血失調 藍, 昏迷 灰 (screen dithered, cannot move until it clears), 走火入魔 紫, 流血 紅. 醉 and 內傷 come only from undated 17173 guides 【待考證】 for 1.0; 內傷 infliction is documented only from 2002-01-25. 麻痺, 封穴, 混亂 are 2002-01-25.
- [corrected] Status details (TW gaoshou/28, 4.htm): 醉 +atk/+def −hit; 內傷 no normal 精氣神 regen, 氣 max drops periodically, cured by opposite-polarity 內功 or a 武館 武師; 走火入魔 cannot recover/use 內力, self-heals in 30 min; 氣血失調 from medicine overuse, slow regen, more medicine can cause 昏迷, clears in 30 min; 流血 氣 max keeps dropping, 敷藥 stops it (30 min later). The CN 2002 guide's 走火入魔 (auto-PK, '40 levels') is a different/later definition. 流血's cause (爆發一擊) was not found 【待考證】. 混亂 (yellow): attacks anyone, 3–5 min, from 靈鷲 attacks scaled by 定性; 麻痺: cannot move, gauge slower, 3–5 min, from 古墓 attacks scaled by 清心; 俠義 resists 毒/麻痺/混亂; **定力 shortens status duration** (official attribute page). 膽識 enables counter-attacks at high values; 殺氣 trades defence for attack; 飽食度 0 slows 精氣神 regen. — gaoshou/28, 4; 17173 2002-04-12 n12_928398; jy1-1-5.
- [corrected] Medicine: 狂暴丹 / 鐵甲丸 (+1–2成 damage/defence for 1 時辰; overuse → 血氣失調); 2001-08-16 medicine restoring 內力 added. The "2001-12-06 per-sect medicine NPC" claim was **not found** in news 299 (unverifiable). — jy1-2-4-7/8; news 317.
- [confirmed] 叛派 triggers 2001-08-07 (news 141): 少林 善惡<−299, PK>3, 殺氣>2000, 同門相殘; 武當 善惡<−99 / <−199 (by NPC group), 同門相殘; 峨嵋 善惡<−299, 同門相殘; 全真 善惡<−299, PK>10, 同門相殘, 結婚; 古墓 善惡<−299, 殺氣>2000, 結婚, PK>3; 華山 善惡<−299, PK>1, 同門相殘; 恆山 善惡<0, PK>1, 殺氣>3000, 同門相殘; 星宿 PK command on 丁春秋; 靈鷲 同門相殘; 血刀 PK command on 血刀老祖. news 317 (2001-08-16): killing a fellow disciple who joined (參戰) your battle does not trigger 叛教; sect-only items are removed on 叛教. — news 141, 317.
- [confirmed] Penalty timeline: until 2001-08-25 an accidental trigger = full betrayal; from 2001-08-25 (news 160) accidental → sect arts −1/5 (no expulsion), deliberate → a new 「叛教」 dialog option, all arts incl. basics halved, hunted by sect-mates; from 2001-11-15 (news 331, explained in news 268 on 11-19) accidental or killing a fellow disciple = 「違反門規」 → **1 game day = 2 real hours** in the 門派監獄 (online time only, no skill loss); deliberate 叛教 still halves everything and makes you 公敵 (3 game days if caught); sect skill → 0 (jy2-22). 2001-11-02: deleting skills or betraying needs password + 暗碼. — news 160, 331, 268, 329; jy2-22.
- [confirmed] Jail (jy1-2-5-4, added 2001-10-05 with 捉拿門派公敵): a 公敵 (betrayed, or 滅派 at another sect) can be hunted via 雜項→捉拿公敵 (a party only catches its own sect's 公敵) or by sect NPCs; on death → 門派監獄 for **3 金庸 days = 6 real hours**; leave by serving the time or by a friend paying **10萬** (贖人); prisoners keep only 傳音入密 and 輕聲細語 (no 拍賣 channel or other commands); the 門派監獄看守人 stands beside the jail and tells the remaining time; release clears the 公敵 mark (改過自新). Jail location (per sect or shared) unknown. — jy1-2-5-4; news 322, 331.
- [corrected] Time scale: 1 game day = 2 real hours; 1 時辰 = 10 real minutes. — news 331.
- [confirmed] 少林 graded enforcement (jy1-1-1-1): light (善惡<−99, PK 1–2, 殺氣重) → masters refuse; 受戒 at 初祖庵; severe (善惡<−299, PK≥3, 殺氣>2000, 同門互殘) → expulsion = 叛教 (本門武功減1/2, never re-enter); enforcer 普知; meat −1 level 佛法; 禁足 (no task → cannot leave). 木人巷: 少林心法, 少林長拳, 少林身法 ≥ 50, no equipment. — jy1-1-1-1.
- [corrected] Three distinct tiers across sects: (a) 停止傳功 (sect-page conditions), (b) 違反門規 → 1 game day jail (jy2-22 list, from 2001-11-15), (c) 逐出師門 = 叛教 (sect page). The two 古墓 lists resolve as: PK>10 / 殺氣>10000 = later jail triggers; PK>3 / −299 / 守宮砂 / marriage = expulsion. The 武當 page adds 同門相殘 incl. NPCs → immediate expulsion, 俠義 reclaimed. — jy2-22; sect pages.
- [confirmed] 公敵: random fights with that sect's disciples, every sect NPC fights you on its map, never rejoin. 古墓: forcing past 孫婆婆 → high-rate battles but no 公敵; fighting 孫婆婆 → 公敵. 武當: a non-member choosing 滅派 at the 知客 → battles everywhere in 武當. — sect pages.
- [confirmed] PK: PK值 > 3 → guards hunt you; 實戰 < 500 cannot be force-PK'd (2001-07-20); 京城半仙 removes PK值 (2001-11-02, 1.0B). Death (2001-07-20): lose 1 level of the highest art only. 逃 only from a marked tile (chance); 無敵落跑鞋 100% from any tile (even chase/PK) except quest battles; chase (追擊) battles cannot be fled (2001-08-30). — jy1-1-5; news 308, 319, 329; jy1-2-4-2.
- [confirmed] 十八銅人陣 (official 少林 page): 3-player party; 6 bronze men at a time, 3 waves, each stronger; reward: free entry to 藏經閣, meet 三長老, who teach the three 金剛伏魔圈; the 羅漢練武師 grants the right after enough 實戰 (number not given: 60,000 in this file's 少林 entry vs 50萬 in a later guide 【待考證】). A 2001-09-14 patch fixed a bug where a party solving 18銅人陣 always lost → live in 1.0. Later guide: 慧潔 entry, 澄觀 fight → 藏經閣. 金剛伏魔圈 lv1 at 基本陣法70 日月鞭法50 佛法70 (2017 relay, later). — jy1-1-1-1; news 321; gonglue/3; steamxo 1122998.
- [confirmed] Encounters (2001-08-30, news 319): NPC attacks became a mix of 追擊 (visible chasing NPCs) and 地雷, to reduce 地雷 frequency; chase battles cannot be fled; NPC reinforcements can join mid-battle; spectating opened. 守護者之煙 (2001-08-09) cuts encounters for 5 min and cannot be used in a party. The original never had an opt-in prompt: the owner-approved 查看偵察 (GDD §5.10) is 【設計提案・非原作】. — news 319, 316.
- Open (mechanics): the exact 2001 command bar (暗 at launch? 閃 effect?); formulas/costs for 毒/醫/暗/點穴; per-throw ammo; old 陣 station layouts and bonus scaling; jail location; poison duration and per-action loss; 內傷/醉 as 1.0 statuses.

---

## 故事包研究 (2001 events, sect story quests, living 江湖; researched and adversarially re-checked 2026-10-19)

> How to read: same format as the M3a section: `[verdict] claim — era — sources`. Verdicts: **confirmed**, **corrected** (the corrected form is written here), **later** (post-1.0A), **unverifiable**, **refuted**. Contract for building this content: `docs/STORY_PLAN.md`. Defaults chosen for the owner are tagged **Owner default** and are repeated in STORY_PLAN §1.3.
> Source caveats that apply to the whole section:
> - **17173 tishi.htm** (「金庸新任務提示和攻略」) is the only primary source for the 2001 teaser blurbs. It has exactly 7 dated blocks: 2001-07-18, 07-26, 08-10, 08-17, 08-31, 11-29, 12-07 (no Sept/Oct block; the 11-29 and 12-07 blocks are 神鵰-period = 1.0B content). Each block lists entries with ◎; one undated block (launch quests) uses `>`. The page's live copy is now simplified Chinese; traditional quotes below are transliterations.
> - **Official TW dates differ by 1–2 days** from 17173 (official 08-08 = 17173 08-10, 08-16 = 08-17, 08-30 = 08-31, 12-06 = 12-07). Owner default: official date canonical, 17173 date recorded as alias.
> - **jy.chinesegamer.net index_jy2-2.asp was never a quest-hint page** (gallery in 2001, ISP list later). Any claim of "official TW 任務提示 text" from it is refuted.
> - **Official chinesegamer quest pages (index_jy3-*)** carry the 15th-expansion 「五嶽劍派」 footer: official but late. **gaoshou/23** (special-NPC coordinates) and **other.htm** are undated (other.htm ≈ Aug–Sep 2001 by its newest link, 2001-09-19; gaoshou/23 lists 郭襄, so possibly post-launch).
> - **Bahamut 紅煉 (2004-09-06)** and **jy2001.net (2019 compilation)** share one author chain; count them as one source. **jyfreeonline (2012–15)** and **jytzst (2024)** are 免費/懷舊-era.
> - No verbatim in-game dialogue survives for **any** event below. All dialogue must be written as 【設計提案】 paraphrase of the blurbs and walkthroughs.
> - Coordinates: original x/y ÷ 24 = `world_pos` (WORLD_2001 §1.1); the y axis direction of the 2001 map is not proven, so "NE of" statements are avoided.

### 劉二爺金盆洗手（上／下）(q_liu_jinpen, q_liu_jinpen_2)

- [confirmed] 上 is in the 2001-07-18 block, ◎, no 解法 link. Blurb: 「衡山派第二把交椅的劉正風決心退隱江湖，正準備大發武林帖給諸位武林同道，素聞莫掌門與劉二爺不和，正巧莫先生不知到了哪去，以致帖子無法傳達。」 ◎ is the default marker on the page, so it is **not** evidence of a sect-only quest. — 1.0A — tishi.htm.
- [confirmed] 上 walkthrough (Bahamut 2004 / jy2001 2019): giver 衡山弟子 at 衡山下; at 申時 go to the seaside 上山路口 and find 莫大 at (25249,13399) = +2609 x / −2869 y from the 衡山 anchor (22640,16268 → [943,678]); return to the same 衡山弟子. Reward **1000兩**; no 名聲/歷練/善惡, no choice, no prerequisite recorded. That the player carries the 武林帖 is an inference from the blurb. — 1.0A event, 2004 guide — forum.gamer.com.tw G2 sn=351; jy2001 t/1876.
- [confirmed, added] An **independent 2001 walkthrough** of the same event exists (17173 2001-08-29 「劉正風事件」 by 上海5-日向小次郎): 劉公子 by the pond opposite the 衡山 gate gives a letter for 劉正風, said to appear at dusk by the big river north of 衡山; at dusk you meet 莫大先生 instead, who says to leave the letter at 衡山; return to 劉公子 → 「1000元」. So the 2001 giver is named **劉公子**, the time is **黃昏**, the 2004 guide says 衡山弟子 / 申時. — 1.0A — web.archive.org 2001-8-29 n215_132057.
- [corrected] GDD.md §8.2 row q_liu_jinpen (giver 【待考證】, 【提案】300兩+50學點) is wrong: reward is 1000兩, no 學點. **Owner default**: giver 劉公子 (a 衡山弟子, 2001 name), 莫大 present at 申 and 酉 (covers 申時 and 黃昏). — project.
- [confirmed] 下 (Bahamut title 劉二爺金盆洗手(下); jy2001 「魯連榮秘密客任務」): requires 上 completed. Giver 魯連榮 (Bahamut: a mid-level 衡山弟子; the 「中級殺手般」 wording is jy2001 only) on 「衡山下面的小島」 over the bridge at the map's lower right; 17173 gaoshou/23 「衡山派魯連榮 23262.18034 衡山下方小島過橋旁邊」 (→ [969,751]). Steps: 魯連榮 talks of a 秘密組織 → choose 「幫他」 then 「明白」 → he gives the password hint → 衡山小茶店, 休息, click 秘密客 (gaoshou/23: 「衡山小茶店右上龍門遺孤旁」, no coordinates) → answer 「太陽月亮..」 then 「剷平他」 (both truncated) → 秘密客 reveals the rumour was 教主故意放出去的消息 to wipe out foolish 正道 → 6–7 NPCs attack; fleeing is acceptable → return to 魯連榮 (Bahamut: he was injured, asks you to wait; jy2001 only: 「我跟他說是魔教的圈套」). Reward **名聲 +40, 江湖歷練 +40**; no silver/items/善惡. — release date 【待考證】 (not in the 2001 log; earliest firm attestation 2004-09-06) — G2 sn=352; jy2001 t/1876; gaoshou/23.
- [unverifiable] Whether fleeing still pays the full +40/+40; the refuse branch at 幫他; ambush composition; whether 莫大 exists outside 申時; repeatability. **Owner defaults**: once per character; flee still completes; 「太陽月亮」 = 日月神教 by implication only; turn-in uses the 「魔教的圈套」 wording; no sect restriction; 下 is shipped as era 1.0A with `src: "later"` and a 【待考證】 release-date note.
- [confirmed] Novel 《笑傲江湖》 ch.6–7: the ceremony is broken up by 嵩山派 (史登達, 費彬, 丁勉, 陸柏, 五嶽令旗) over 劉正風's friendship with 曲洋; 魯連榮 (金眼雕) is present; 莫大 is estranged. The game's 魔教 秘密客 ambush is game canon, not novel. — novel.

### 孤鴻子師伯的死因 (q_guhongzi, 峨嵋)

- [confirmed] 2001-07-18 block: 「靜觀師姐的家裡發生了一些事，想請玩家幫忙送封家書回去，卻因此牽連出峨嵋派與明教的恩怨」, no 解法 link (4 of the 7 ◎ entries in that block have none). — 1.0A — tishi.htm.
- [confirmed] 2001 walkthrough (17173 2001-09-01 by 殺佛, and 2001-09-02 GS1 guide): 靜觀 asks you to take a 家書 to 雲兒 in 西夏 (「西夏進城左邊第一個屋子」) → 雲兒's reply → 靜觀 reveals 明教 activity near 大理 and how 孤鴻子 died → ask around 大理 (2001: 礦石大王, 王大富人, 李裁縫, 樵夫; 2021 video: villagers near the 裝備店) → 「大理左面的沙漠洞窟」, go far left → fight the 明教分舵主 (video: click 詭異的人; win; click again) → he 「並不壞」 → report to 靜觀 → reward from 滅絕 「以後就可以和她學劍了，但要你等級達到要求」. — 1.0A 2001-09 — 2001-9-1 n959_875542; 2001-9-2 n589_506081; YouTube SQow-9z0DZo.
- [confirmed, later numbers] Later guide: prereq 峨嵋心法5 / 實戰1000 / 名聲100; 雲兒 gives 3 清水 + 5 牛肉餅 + 雲兒的信; reward 學點50 / 名聲10 / 江湖歷練10. GDD §8.2 「【提案】200 學點」 should change to these, tagged later guide. — 2012+ — jy2001 t/300.
- [corrected] The quest and the 2001 blurb call her **靜觀師姐**; data/npcs.json names `n_em_jingguan` 「靜觀師太」. **Owner default**: dialogue says 師姐, NPC name unchanged (rename left to owner).
- [corrected] Novel 《倚天屠龍記》: 孤鴻子 borrowed 倚天劍 from 滅絕, lost to 楊逍 before drawing it; 楊逍 threw it aside as scrap; 孤鴻子 fell ill on the road home and died; officials seized the sword. 「活活氣死」 is a paraphrase. The serialized version had a 孤鴻子–滅絕 romance (師兄妹). The video's 「師伯與師姪的禁忌之戀(?)」 is a joke; **do not build on it**. **Owner default**: 靜觀 tells the novel version, 【設計提案】.
- [later] The 2019 comments place a 大理洞 at post-revision coordinates (「地圖改版，大理地區面目全非」). **Owner default**: a proposal cave room west of 大理 (`dlw_desertcave`), no coordinates.
- [corrected] 替天行道: the existing note 「fan-only」 is wrong. 2001-09-01 has 靜觀 issue 替天行道令 甲/乙/丙 (殺人王 衡山城外; 強盜大當家 at the foot of the mountain left of 大理; 丙 = the 明教 hunt). 2001-09-02 titles a 4-target chain 「連環殺人案」 (殺人王, 土匪大當家, 惡賊 in 高昌迷宮, 淫賊 near 雁門關 → 斷惡 +500; gate 峨嵋心法 or a 峨嵋 art ≥ 20). **Owner default**: ship 「連環殺人案」 (alias 替天行道) as the repeatable 斷惡 sect quest without the 高昌迷宮 target (1.0B area), keep the 明教 hunt inside q_guhongzi; repeat status 【待考證】.

### 尋找虛竹 (q_xuzhu, 少林)

- [confirmed] 2001-07-18 block, verbatim: 「道品師兄因虛竹師弟下山去，有好一陣子沒有消息而非常擔心，於是指派少林弟子下山去找尋他的消息」. The date is the mainland update post; "1.0A" is our label. — tishi.htm (2004-04-21 snapshot).
- [confirmed] 2001 walkthrough (17173 「虛竹的任務的解法」, 作者未知, 2001-7-23): 道品 (「一進少林左邊房子」; ends as 「道品和尚」) → 虛竹 in 西夏 「城裡左上（公告欄邊上）」 → he asks to be taken to the 皇宮 → at the palace gate talk to the **left** guard, choose 1, wait for his reply, choose 2 → battle → **flee** → back to 虛竹 「就給你回信了」 → 道品 → **3 大還丹** 「（精，氣，神，都補300點，賣藥店1400元一個）」. — 1.0A — web.archive.org 2001-7-23 n996_912972.
- [corrected] 大還丹 restores 300 精/氣/神 (not permanent); 「賣藥店1400元一個」 reads as the drug-shop **buy** price (medium-high confidence). 「回信」 is not named as an item.
- [unverifiable] What winning or losing the guard fight does; dialogue text; non-少林 access; 善惡/佛法 changes; repeatability; 時辰 gates. **Owner defaults**: 少林 disciples only; flee completes the step, a win also completes it 【設計提案】, a loss follows normal death rules; quest item 「虛竹的回信」 【設計提案】 consumed on turn-in; one-time; reward only 3 大還丹; 大還丹 sold at 1400 in 西夏 藥鋪.
- [later] ali213 2020: 「少林小僧-虛竹(3445,799)」 in 西夏 for the 靈鷲 八荒 chain. Do not merge with the 2001 position.
- [confirmed] Novel 《天龍八部》: 虛竹 left 少林 delivering 英雄帖, ended up in the 西夏 palace (童姥's ice cellar; later the 招駙馬). 少林 generations around him are 靈/玄/慧/虛, so 道品 is game-original.

### 藍鳳凰採收毒物／藍鳳凰事件 (q_lanfeng)

- [confirmed] 2001-07-18 block blurb (17173 tishi.htm; **[corrected, M3b]** the official TW site has it too — index_jy3-2-2, see the M3b 未做的 2001 事件 section; the earlier note「17173 only, the official site never had this text」was wrong): 「藍鳳凰急欲製造五寶花蜜酒，因而一直在收集其所需之五毒，不料在幫藍鳳凰收集五毒的途中，又得知幫忙收集五毒的瑩月兒原來跟華山派的藍依涵是多年的好友，在收集散落在廣大江湖中的毒物同時，也幫幫瑩月兒吧。」 Same block: 出外抓毒物 (星宿 咸楓子). — 1.0A — tishi.htm.
- [confirmed] 藍鳳凰 (藍妹妹) at (6877,14787) 「雪山以東的對岸」 (→ [287,616]). 2001 animal spots: 蜈蚣 「雪山北方」 (5003,13465; the inline text says 53xx); 青蛇 「大理的北方還沒過橋的左方小樹林」 (6008,17294); 蝎子 「星宿的西南方島的左上橋附近」 (2645,5537); 蜘蛛 「恆山東北方」 (13150,8182; 2004: 16552,5192). 蟾蜍: 「向藍妹妹東南方走，找螢月兒她會給你五張麻布，要你送去華山找藍依涵（在華山大廳外右邊）再回來找螢月兒便可以得到五隻蟾蜍」 (瑩月兒 11555,14781; the 07-21 article says 5xxx). 「1--4都要打5次。並在裝備裡的其他欄檢查是否有5隻」. Later-guide animal coordinates are a different (possibly sub-map) set. — 1.0A — 2001-7-21 n528_445091 (flourx); 2001-8-2 n820_736977; 2001-8-20 n453_369347.
- [confirmed] 2001 reward: 「一灌藥以及基本毒術升一級」 (medicine unnamed). 2004+: the toad hand-off gives 名聲+5 歷練+15. 台免: 基毒熟練+50, 善惡−100, 恆山派 get only 5 解毒藥; 福緣22 gate, 基本毒術 < 30, wait about 1 game day before 藍鳳凰 accepts the hand-in, redo after 3 h. jyfree/jy2001: 福緣23, summary +200 vs step +50 (self-contradictory). — 2001 / 2004 / 2011–13.
- [confirmed] 藍鳳凰事件 (2001-08-10 block): 「…若是好心幫忙藍鳳凰收集毒物後，藍鳳凰會送非常好的解毒聖品或是教導一些毒術的能力…」. Its 解法 link is a title search returning only the three poison-collecting articles. Plausibly a revision / reward branch of the same quest (medium).
- **Owner defaults**: one quest `q_lanfeng` (1.0A); reward 基本毒術 +1 level + 1 解毒藥 (`c_jieduyao`, the unnamed 「一灌藥」 【設計提案】); 恆山派 branch 5 解毒藥 instead of 毒術 (台免, src later); 善惡 −100 src later; 福緣 22 gate src later; 1-game-day wait before hand-in and 1-game-day cooldown src later; 瑩月兒 toad step 名聲+5 歷練+15 (2004); each of the four animal spots = 5 fights (2001); reuse `m_xh_snake`/`m_xh_scorpion`, new weak 蜈蚣/蜘蛛 beasts 【設計提案】; 藍鳳凰 alone in the field, 藍依涵 outside the 華山 hall on the right, 瑩月兒 stays at `em_teahouse`.
- [confirmed] Characters: 藍鳳凰 = 五毒教主 (《笑傲江湖》); the official NPC page quoted for her is a 五嶽劍派-era page (background only). 瑩月兒 and 藍依涵 are game-original; 痴情的梁發 (2001-07-26) has 梁發 in love with 瑩月兒, so her existing proposal talk line (waiting for someone from 大理) should be reconciled.

### 雲兒小姐的煩惱 (q_yuner)

- [corrected] Date: the quest is a **launch-era** 西夏 newbie quest (in the undated `>` block; described by 独孤求寒 on **2001-07-17**, before the 07-18 update). GDD date 2001-07-18 → 「開服／≤ 2001-07-17」. — 2001-7-17 n431_348015; tishi.htm.
- [confirmed] Blurb: 「在西夏的民房內，雲兒小姐正在為了要嫁給誰而煩惱，到底是要選在藥局工作的李台安呢？還是從小一起生活的青梅竹馬阿寶呢？」 Walkthrough (2001-07-23): 雲兒 「一進城左邊房子」 → 李台安 「在城內藥店老闆旁邊」 → 礦工阿寶 「在西夏城外西邊（7277，1027）」 → back to 雲兒, she asks you to choose → choose 阿寶 → back to 阿寶 → **3 紫石礦 + 3 綠石礦**. Two 2001 sources agree on the 阿寶 reward. — 2001-7-23 n274_190972; 2001-7-17.
- [corrected] 李台安 branch: 「選李。。什麼也沒有只有10元和開始給你的一個傷藥」. The 傷藥 may be the starting-kit potion. **Owner default**: 雲兒 pays 10兩 when 李台安 is chosen, no quest 傷藥.
- [confirmed] 阿寶 is the same 礦工阿寶 of 路痴的礦工 (launch block). No 善惡/俠義/名聲 change is recorded (the same author records 俠義 elsewhere, so absence is weak evidence). **Owner defaults**: no alignment change; once per character, choice permanent; independent of 路痴的礦工; 雲兒 is also the 孤鴻子 letter recipient. Original game content (not from a novel).

### 行俠仗義（靈芝商人） (q_xingxia)

- [confirmed] 2001-07-18 block: 「要去大理做靈芝買賣的商人，剛到雁門關就被搶了，附近卻沒有人．．．」, no 解法 link. other.htm (≈ Aug–Sep 2001): 「靈芝商人任務 | 雁門關外的靈芝商人要你從盜匪手中拿回寶盒. | 先和靈芝商對話, 接完任務後在下方不遠處找到盜匪, 取回寶盒交給商人. | 不詳 | 每次三粒靈芝草, 另加名聲和歷練.」 The link between the two names is content-based (no source states it). — 1.0A — tishi.htm; other.htm.
- [confirmed] gaoshou/23: 靈芝商人 20078.2310 「雁門關口」 (→ [837,96], outside the pass); 強盜 20196.3760 「靈芝商人右下橋附近」 (→ [842,157]); 淫魔 below the 強盜; 苗人鳳 「雁門關外右下」; 胡斐, 阿朱 「雁門關內」. — undated, probably 2001.
- [confirmed] Numbers 名聲 +5 / 歷練 +5 come only from 2019 jy2001 and jytzst. 「打一場，打贏」 appears only in 2019+ texts. 「每次」 implies repeatable. GDD 【提案】善惡 +20 has no support.
- [later] 馬王洞 (「靈芝商人左方靠山」; 2019) and 史沖 (2022) are not attested for 1.0A.
- **Owner defaults**: id `q_xingxia`, title 行俠仗義, alias 靈芝商人任務; reward 3 靈芝草 (verified) + 名聲 +5 + 歷練 +5 (src later); remove 善惡 +20; repeatable with a 1-game-day cooldown 【設計提案】; one 強盜 fight 【設計提案】; 靈芝商人 at 雁門關口, 強盜 by the bridge SE; 雁門關 opened as a small walkable region for this (and for 胡斐).

### 解救文泰來／愛喝酒的張豹／大理王捕頭 (q_wentailai)

- [confirmed] 2001-07-26 block lists 解救文泰來 (「紅花會四當家文泰來被朝廷的人抓走了，紅花會一行人正為解救他而大傷腦筋...」), 大理王捕頭 (received an official document about arresting someone) and 愛喝酒的張豹 (give the 西夏 drunkard wine to learn news) — three facets of one chain. — tishi.htm.
- [confirmed] 2001 walkthrough (「愛喝酒的張豹任務的解法」, 2001-8-2): buy one 白酒 and one 羊乳酒 at the inn → 張豹 to the right of the 西夏 衙門, next to 虛竹 (gone at 子時), treat him → leave 西夏 to the lower left, 陳家洛 asks you to gather intelligence → 張豹 says he talks for wine; **only then** buy one bottle of every inn wine (earlier purchases do not count) → pick option 2 → 王捕頭 at the 大理 衙門 → back to 張豹, buy again → 「王捕頭的情報」 → do what he says, buy again, last option → report to 陳家洛. No reward stated. — 2001-8-2 n214_130547.
- [later] Official 張豹任務 page (15th-expansion era): gates 實戰500 / 名聲100 / 善惡100; wines 狀元紅×3, 紹興×3, 竹葉青×3; 【我只是好奇來問問文泰來的消息的】; bring 3 美酒狀元紅 to 王捕頭, once drunk you get 「偷抄的一份公文手抄本」 → 陳家洛 → **江湖歷練 +10**; 陳家洛 at 百花村. — index_jy3-4.
- [confirmed] Nobody frees 文泰來 in any source; the quest ends with intelligence. 陳家洛 at 6397,3527 「西夏城外」 (gaoshou/23); 鐵心網's 「舊西夏城外」 is later naming.
- **Owner defaults**: 2001 flow (白酒 + 羊乳酒, then one of each inn wine) + the later 公文手抄本 object + 歷練 +10 tagged 【後期版】; no stat gate (2001 lists none); 陳家洛 stays at `xw_road` (existing placement; noted deviation from [267,147]); one-time.

### 華山內奸（勞德諾）→ 紫霞神功 (q_hs_spy, with 劍氣宗之別 q_hs_jianqi)

- [corrected] Blurb (2001-08-10 block; official 08-08), complete: 「在西夏客棧的勞德諾居然是內奸，那他到底又是誰派來的內奸呢，而令狐沖早就已經懷疑他很久了，到底是什麼事呢，若岳不群知道他是華山氣宗的內奸又會如何呢，所以快去西夏的客棧看看勞德諾有什麼異常吧，不過華山心法可要多加熟練喔。」
- [corrected] Prerequisites: 華山心法50, 讀書30, 悟性30, 儒風30 (article 二, 徐悅瀧; 「用不著達到60」, a reprint from 金庸遊戲網); article 一 used 儒風60 and suggests testing 心法30/儒風30; KFLearn: 華心50 讀書40 儒風60 悟30. You must first meet the 劍宗 entry conditions and **refuse** 成不憂 in the 後山 cave, or 勞德諾 ignores you. After learning 紫霞 you can never join 劍宗. — 2001-8-27 n930_846291, n162_78514; gaoshou/22.
- [corrected] Steps (article 二 order, default): 勞德諾 at the 西夏客店 (special dialogue) → 令狐沖 at 17461,8715 by the pool between 華山 and 武當, present 巳/午/未 (the 子時 比武場 令狐沖 gives 尋找任盈盈 instead) → back to 勞德諾, 「敷衍一下」, learn his link to 「衡山左冷蟬」 → 令狐沖 again → 華山密函 → 岳不群 → 追擊令 → kill/defeat 勞德諾 at the 西夏 inn → **氣宗武譜** (article 一 says 劍宗武譜; 2 of 3 sources say 氣宗) → 岳不群 → 紫霞神功 (self-train only). 「衡山左冷蟬」 appears in both the 2001 article and the later blog: keep it as in-game wording (novel: 嵩山 左冷禪).
- [confirmed] 劍宗 entry (2001-08-02): 華山劍法35, 心法30 → 思過崖 cave → 成不憂 asks whether you know 劍宗 → yes; a wrong click loses the chance. Refusing makes you 氣宗-eligible. — 2001-8-2 n732_648232.
- [confirmed] No 善惡/名聲/歷練 change in any source; GDD 「【提案】儒風熟練」 is unsupported. Reward = the 紫霞 unlock and permanent 氣宗.
- [later] jyfreeonline: 令狐沖 at the 華山 NE mine (24790,6965), also 辰時4刻; 華山搜查令; the fight 「不用真打」; the 「65級」 is a 消點 cap at 英白羅, not a prerequisite.
- **Owner defaults**: gate 華心50 讀書30 悟30 儒風30 (article 二); 令狐沖 at `hy_pond` (華山水池, matches 「華山與武當之間的水池」) during 巳午未; fight must be won; item 氣宗武譜; flag `hs_qizong` set by refusing 成不憂, `hs_jianzong` by accepting (劍宗 content itself is out of this package beyond the flag); `hs_zixia` art added as QUEST tier.

### 尋找楊康 (q_yangkang, 全真)

- [confirmed] 2001-08-31 block (official 08-30): 「邱處機與江南七怪打了個賭，他得先找到楊鐵心的後人―楊康，教導他武功；十八年後與江南七怪所教之郭嘯天後人較量武功，比試高下。如果自認有能力可代丘處機去找尋楊康，那就去試試...」 (the source spells both 邱 and 丘). The 全真 catalog has 「楊康與郭靖」 and 「丘處機與江南七怪」. — tishi.htm; gonglue/6; index_jy3-1-8.
- [confirmed] 2001-09-07 全真 guide: 馬鈺手諭 = 給七怪的友善信息 (柯鎮惡); 長春子挑戰書 → 柯鎮惡; 「郭靖目前沒有開放 2001/9/4」. gaoshou/23: 楊康 「西夏皇宮，進密室第一個路口右拐，就看見了！」; 江南七怪 「恆山派上方的雪地上」. — 2001-9-7 n881_797753.
- [unverifiable] No walkthrough exists anywhere. [later] jyfreeonline: giver 王志明; 養生20 善惡500 歷練300 → 丘處機手諭 → fight 楊康 in the 西夏皇宮 → 郭靖匕首 → 歷練50 學點10.
- **Owner defaults**: giver 丘處機 (`n_qz_qiuchuji`), 全真 disciples only; 丘處機手諭 → walk to the 西夏皇宮 secret passage → fight 楊康 → 郭靖匕首 → back to 丘處機; reward 歷練50 學點10 (src later); the 柯鎮惡 / 郭靖 half stays closed.

### 幫助陳大寶 (q_chendabao, 武當 俠義)

- [confirmed] 2001-07-26 blurb: 西夏的陳大寶 fled bandits, heard near 終南山 that 武當 values 俠義; 「武當弟子如有在終南山附近看到陳大寶的人可以幫助他」 (武當-only is implied by the teaser, not stated by the walkthrough). 陳大寶 at 16845,10507 「終南山左上方河邊」 (existing `n_qz_chendabao` at `zn_river`). — tishi.htm; gaoshou/23.
- [confirmed] 2001-08-02 walkthrough: 陳大寶 (looks like a villager) → 討伐令 → the 山賊 past the bridge at the far upper left of the 西夏 map, a single fight against the 賊頭 (暴猛烂) → 青雪寨令牌 → 陳大寶 → 「俠義值暴增2-300(保守估計)」. 青雪寨 lies between 青城 and 雪山; follow-ups: 小嘍囉 in front of the 大理客棧 → 熊霸天 beside the 青城 farmland → 獨眼仁 above the 雪山; the 令牌 disappears after talking; all 4 ≈ +1000 俠義 (「從俠義10級到14級又多200多」); 「如此才算完成才能一直接此任務」 (repeatable afterwards). — 2001-8-2 n729_645897.
- [confirmed] 鐵心網 FAQ (late 2001, marked NEW): 「直接PK陳大寶, 十分鐘可以降到負500.」 Per-kill amount 【待考證】.
- **Owner defaults**: 武當 only; 4 stages, 俠義 熟練 +250 for the first fight and +250 each later (≈ +1000) 【設計提案 scaled from 「2-300」】; repeatable after completion with a 1-game-day cooldown 【設計提案】; 陳大寶 attackable (`fight` service) with 善惡 −50 per win 【設計提案】; spots: 賊頭 at `ts_road1` (upper-left bridge), 小嘍囉 at `dl_square`, 熊霸天 at `dlw_fork` (青城 does not exist in 1.0 map), 獨眼仁 at `xd_road2`.

### Other 2001 events with archived 2001 walkthroughs (priority P2)

- [confirmed] 迷路的江湖賣藝商人 (07-18): 20141,14501 below 劍冢 → letter to the 婦人 in 大理 (2310,1518) → 名聲+3 歷練+7. — 2001-8-2 n389_305229.
- [confirmed] 痴情的梁發 (07-26): 梁發 on 華山 → answer 「有....」 → letter to 瑩月兒 (11555,14780) → reply → 梁發學武函 (purpose 【待考證】, maybe the 伏虎拳 key). — 2001-8-2 n200_116219.
